\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 1}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{The Incarnation and Sufferings of the Son of God}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 1}}
    \\
    \textit{C. M. TALLIS}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BLEST be the Wisdom and the Power, \\
        The Justice and the Grace, \\
        That joined in council to restore \\
        And save our ruined race.

    \item Our father ate forbidden fruit, \\
        And from his glory fell; \\
        And we his children thus were brought \\
        To death and near to hell.

    \item Blest be the Lord who sent His Son, \\
        To take our flesh and blood, \\
        To bring us life He gave His own, \\
        And made our peace with God.

    \item He honoured all His Father's laws \\
        Which we have disobeyed; \\
        He bore our sins upon the cross, \\
        And our full ransom paid.

    \item Behold Him rising from the grave, \\
        Behold Him raised on high; \\
        He pleads His merit there to save \\
        Transgressors doomed to die.

    \item There on a glorious throne He reigns, \\
        And by His power divine \\
        Redeems us from the slavish chains \\
        Of Satan and of sin.

    \item Thence soon the Son of God shall come, \\
        And with archangel voice \\
        Shall call His saints forth from the tomb \\
        While living saints rejoice.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 2}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. KELLY}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item Lo! the infant Saviour lies, \\
        He appears in lowly guise, \\
        Yet by faith we read the words--- \\
        King of kings and Lord of lords.

    \item See! He stands at Pilate's bar, \\
        Most despised of all by far; \\
        Still to Him belong the words--- \\
        King of kings and Lord of lords.

    \item He who wears the crown of thorns, \\
        He whom man reviles and scorns, \\
        Yet demands as His the words--- \\
        King of kings and Lord of lords.

    \item On the cross 'tis still the same, \\
        Never can He yield His claim \\
        To those ever glorious words--- \\
        King of kings and Lord of lords.

    \item Past the conflict of His love, \\
        See He takes His place above; \\
        On His vesture shine the words--- \\
        King of kings and Lord of lords.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 3}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.7. NEANDER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item ON this Lordly day assembled, \\
        Gathered by the call of God, \\
        We rejoice, though once we trembled, \\
        Fearing His avenging rod; \\
        Christ redeemed us; \\
        Let us trace the path He trod:

    \item In the Babe in David's city, \\
        Lowly in a manger laid, \\
        We behold the Lord Almighty, \\
        Him by whom the world was made. \\
        What a wonder, \\
        God in human form arrayed!

    \item Mark the earnest Workman toiling, \\
        Faithful to His task and true, \\
        Deeming nought of labour soiling \\
        That His kingly hands would do. \\
        Man so perfect; \\
        Holy, noble, humble too.

    \item View the Man of Sorrows, treading \\
        Earth's dark vale of sin and woe, \\
        Love and healing mercy spreading \\
        Everywhere His footsteps go. \\
        Pattern Servant, \\
        Doing all God's will below.

    \item Now upon the cross suspended, \\
        See the Saviour crucified; \\
        Slain by men His love befriended, \\
        Who His agonies deride. \\
        Yet for sinners, \\
        'Neath the wrath of God He died.

    \item His atoning work completed, \\
        Now He lives no more to die; \\
        On the Father's throne He's seated, \\
        See Him victor-crowned on high. \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        'Tis by Him we now draw nigh.

    \item For His birth and life obedient \\
        To Thy perfect holy will, \\
        For His death, no mere expedient, \\
        But Thy counsels to fulfil, \\
        We Thy people, \\
        Bless\`ed God, would praise Thee still.

    \item Praise the Father, for He gave us \\
        Gift most precious in His Son. \\
        Praise the Son, for He to save us \\
        Such a mighty work has done. \\
        Praise the Spirit \\
        Who revealed it; \\
        Praise Jehovah, Three in One. \\
        Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 4}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. ALL FOR JESUS}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item FROM the realm of light and glory \\
        Came the Son of God as Man, \\
        To unfold the wondrous story \\
        Veiled in God's eternal plan.

    \item Leaving heav\`en's throne of splendour, \\
        Stepping down to earth's dark night--- \\
        Shepherds find Him in a manger, \\
        Gaze in wonder at the sight!

    \item Woman's Seed in Eden promised, \\
        God incarnate, virgin-born; \\
        Morning Star by men unnoticed, \\
        Herald of a glorious dawn.

    \item As Jehovah's Servant treading \\
        Sin-stained earth with heavenly grace, \\
        Ever onward, never faltering--- \\
        All His steps we love to trace.

    \item On the cross, His will unshaken, \\
        Dying, outcast, crushed, alone; \\
        Visage marred, by God forsaken, \\
        There for sinners to atone.

    \item Victor over death and Hades, \\
        Foes all vanquished 'neath His feet, \\
        To the Father's side He rises, \\
        There to take the highest seat!

    \item Tribute bring we to the Father \\
        For the Son, His gift of love; \\
        Bowing low, our praise we offer \\
        In the holy place above.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 5}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M.D. BLESSED NAME}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE bless and praise Thee, gracious God, \\
        For giving Thine own Son, \\
        Who did partake of flesh and blood, \\
        In all our sorrows one. \\
        Well may we wonder at the thought \\
        That Christ came down so low, \\
        That He so near to us was brought \\
        To understand our woe.

    \item He, mingling with the human race, \\
        Saw sin, death, Satan's power; \\
        Before His holy piercing gaze \\
        The very demons cower. \\
        But not alone in living strife \\
        He fought the triple foe; \\
        Upon the cross He gave His life; \\
        By dying laid them low.

    \item For us He died upon the tree, \\
        For us was shed His blood, \\
        But more than that since unto Thee \\
        He gave Himself, O God. \\
        Christ, spotless, offered Thee Himself, \\
        Oh, what a gift divine! \\
        Its fragrant worth no tongue can tell, \\
        What joy, O God, was Thine!

    \item In the eternal Spirit He \\
        Offered Himself to God; \\
        And in the Holy Spirit we \\
        Now sing a grateful ode. \\
        For now He lives, we praise Thy name, \\
        He lives no more to die; \\
        'Tis ours to celebrate His fame, \\
        Who sits with Thee on high.

    \item Before Thy face we own His grace, \\
        He once for us was poor. \\
        We bless His name, we sing His fame, \\
        He liveth evermore. \\
        Our hymn of praise with joy we raise, \\
        His worth no tongue can tell. \\
        So great His love all praise above, \\
        He hath done all things well.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 6}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. ST. ALPHEGE}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SENT from th' eternal Father \\
        Who dwells in light above, \\
        Came forth the Son most holy \\
        To manifest His love.

    \item This is the wondrous story--- \\
        Born of a Hebrew maid \\
        The Lord of life and glory; \\
        And in a manger laid.

    \item He grew in human stature, \\
        In every childlike grace; \\
        He lived a life most holy \\
        In all His youthful days;

    \item Attaining manhood's ripeness, \\
        Midst sinners sinless He; \\
        And though in human likeness, \\
        From human errors free.

    \item He entered on His life-work, \\
        Approved by God Most High; \\
        When tempted in the desert \\
        He made the Tempter fly.

    \item In Galilee behold Him \\
        A simple wedding-guest, \\
        But mighty powers enfold Him, \\
        Believing ones are blest.

    \item Through this dark vale of sorrow \\
        He, clothed with pity, went. \\
        In Him each sick and sad one \\
        Found power and mercy blent.

    \item But looming black before Him \\
        See His dread cross appear; \\
        Oh, well may we adore Him, \\
        He bore our sorrows there!

    \item Then glory to the Father, \\
        For us His Son He gave; \\
        To Christ, Kinsman-Redeemer, \\
        He died our souls to save;

    \item To God th' eternal Spirit, \\
        'Tis He reveals the Son; \\
        We praise Thy holy merit, \\
        Jehovah, Three in One.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 7}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. BOSTON}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item 'Tis midnight, and on Olive's brow \\
        The star is dimmed that lately shone; \\
        'Tis midnight in the garden now, \\
        The suffering Saviour prays alone.

    \item 'Tis midnight, and from all removed \\
        The Saviour wrestles 'lone with fears; \\
        E'en that disciple whom He loved \\
        Heeds not his Master's grief and tears.

    \item 'Tis midnight, agonized He kneels, \\
        The sweat falls down like drops of blood; \\
        Yet He who such deep anguish feels \\
        Is not forsaken by His God.

    \item 'Tis midnight, but before Him loom \\
        Yet darker hours of deeper woe; \\
        Golgotha's anguish, death, the tomb, \\
        The patient Saviour needs must know.

    \item O God, those midnight scenes of woe \\
        In tenderest memory we recall; \\
        We bless the One who loved us so, \\
        And fully own that Christ is all.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 8}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. REST}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE Son of God, the Prince of life, \\
        Thrice in the garden prayed; \\
        The sword was drawn to pierce the One \\
        On whom our sins were laid.

    \item He asked, if it were possible, \\
        The cup might pass away; \\
        Made flesh for us, the Son of God \\
        A prostrate suppliant lay.

    \item Strong crying, tears and sweat like blood \\
        Bespeak His agony, \\
        Yet must He sink in deeper grief \\
        That we might never die.

    \item The sword awakened cannot rest \\
        Till God has slain His Son; \\
        The Christ must die on Golgotha \\
        For my sin to atone.

    \item Go to Gethsemane, my soul, \\
        Watch with the Saviour there; \\
        Ponder His pretaste of the cup, \\
        Then to the cross repair.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 9*}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. EDEN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BEHOLD a spotless Victim dies, \\
        Our surety on the cross; \\
        The Lamb of God the sacrifice, \\
        He gave Himself for us.

    \item Whatever curse was ours He bore, \\
        The wormwood and the gall; \\
        There in that lone mysterious hour \\
        Our cup He drained it all.

    \item The Lord Himself and none beside \\
        Its bitterness could know, \\
        Nor other tell the joy's full tide \\
        That from that cup shall flow.

    \item The joy is His, yet wondrous grace, \\
        That joy to us is given; \\
        Our joy to His sore grief we trace, \\
        His death brings life and heaven.

    \item And, while the ages roll along, \\
        This shall our glory be, \\
        And this the new and endless song--- \\
        His love to us, to me.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 10*}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. ST. AGNES, DURHAM}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE Lamb of God to slaughter led, \\
        The King of glory see, \\
        A crown of thorns upon His head; \\
        They nail Him to the tree.

    \item The Father gives His only Son, \\
        The King of glory dies \\
        For us the guilty and undone, \\
        A spotless sacrifice.

    \item Thy name is holy, O our God, \\
        Before Thy throne we bow; \\
        The holy place is our abode, \\
        We know Thy mercy now.

    \item Enthroned with Thee now sits the Lord, \\
        And in Thy bosom dwells. \\
        Justice that smote Him with the sword \\
        Our perfect pardon seals.

    \item Eternal death was once our doom, \\
        Now death has lost its sting; \\
        Triumphant through His empty tomb, \\
        Thy power and love we sing.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 11}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.8.7. ST. JUDE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Line 3 to be repeated]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GAZING on the Lord in glory \\
        While our hearts in worship bow, \\
        There we read the wondrous story \\
        Of the cross, its shame and woe.

    \item Every mark of dark dishonour \\
        Heaped upon the thorn-crowned brow, \\
        All the depths of His heart's sorrow \\
        Told in answering glory now.

    \item On that cross alone, forsaken, \\
        Where no pitying eye was found; \\
        Now to God's right hand exalted--- \\
        With His praise the heavens resound.

    \item Did His God e'en then forsake Him, \\
        Hide Himself from His deep need? \\
        In that face, once marred and smitten, \\
        All His glory now we read.

    \item Rise, our hearts, and bless the Father; \\
        Ceaseless song e'en here begun--- \\
        Endless praise and adoration \\
        To the Father and the Son. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 12*}}
    \\
    \textit{8.6.8.6.8.6. SPOHR}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item How deep and grievous was the woe \\
        Of Christ upon the cross! \\
        It laid the mighty Saviour low \\
        When hanging there for us. \\
        To bring us gain He bare the pain, \\
        And suffered shame and loss.

    \item The wage of sin was in our cup, \\
        Wrath filled it to the brim; \\
        Its bitterness we'll never sup, \\
        That cup was drunk by Him. \\
        'Twas all for us He suffered thus, \\
        That we might live through Him.

    \item There rose a keen relentless storm, \\
        It burst on Christ alone; \\
        It marred His visage and His form, \\
        But thus He saved His own. \\
        He bore the grief for our relief, \\
        Unaided and alone.

    \item Jehovah's righteous anger dire \\
        Fell on His only Son; \\
        He bare the brunt of all God's ire, \\
        And thus our peace was won. \\
        The stripes were sore the Saviour bore; \\
        God spar\`ed not His Son.

    \item His wrathful sword was raised to smite \\
        The One who pleased Him well; \\
        On Him in whom He had delight \\
        The bitter chastening fell. \\
        Our peace He made, the price He paid; \\
        His love no tongue can tell.

    \item It was for us Christ Jesus died, \\
        A Victim on the cross, \\
        Revealing thus the boundless tide \\
        Of love He has to us. \\
        He is our Lord alone adored \\
        Who died upon the cross.

    \item The One who suffered grief and shame \\
        Is raised and glorified. \\
        We sing the triumph of His name \\
        Who groaned and bled and died. \\
        We celebrate His triumph great; \\
        He's raised and glorified.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 13}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. ROCKINGHAM}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item As we survey the wondrous cross \\
        On which the Lord of glory died, \\
        Our richest gains we count but loss, \\
        And pour contempt on all our pride.

    \item Forbid that we should ever boast \\
        Save in the cross of Christ, O God: \\
        All the vain things that charmed us most, \\
        We sacrifice them to His blood.

    \item See from His head, His hands, His feet, \\
        Sorrow and love flow mingled down; \\
        Did e'er such love and sorrow meet, \\
        Or thorns compose so rich a crown?

    \item Were the whole realm of nature ours, \\
        That were an offering far too small; \\
        Love that transcends our highest powers \\
        Demands our heart, our life, our all.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 14}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. ST. SYLVESTER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item STRICKEN, smitten and afflicted, \\
        Lo, He dies upon the tree; \\
        'Tis the Christ by man rejected, \\
        Son of God, 'tis He, 'tis He!

    \item 'Tis the long-expected Saviour, \\
        David's Son and David's Lord, \\
        Sacrificed to show us favour \\
        And declare the love of God.

    \item Mark the sacrifice appointed, \\
        See who bears the awful load; \\
        'Tis the Word, the Lord's Anointed, \\
        Son of Man and Son of God!

    \item Lamb of God, for sinners wounded, \\
        Sacrifice which cancels guilt; \\
        None shall ever be confounded \\
        Who on Him their hopes have built.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 15}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. D. ABERYSTWYTH}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item NAILED upon Golgotha's tree, \\
        Faint and bleeding, who is He? \\
        Hands and feet so rudely torn, \\
        Wreathed with crown of twisted thorn. \\
        Once He lived in heaven above, \\
        Happy in His Father's love; \\
        Son of God, 'tis He, 'tis He, \\
        Nailed upon Golgotha's tree.

    \item Nailed upon Golgotha's tree, \\
        Mocked and taunted, who is He? \\
        Scorners tell Him to come down, \\
        Claim His kingdom and His crown. \\
        He it was who came to bless, \\
        Full of love and tenderness; \\
        Son of Man, 'tis He, 'tis He, \\
        Nailed upon Golgotha's tree.

    \item Nailed upon Golgotha's tree, \\
        As a Victim, who is He? \\
        Bearing sin, but not His own, \\
        Suffering agony unknown. \\
        He, the promised sacrifice, \\
        For the sinner bleeds and dies; \\
        Lamb of God, 'tis He, 'tis He, \\
        Nailed upon Golgotha's tree.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 16}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.8.8.8. STELLA}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O LOVE divine, what hast thou done! \\
        The Christ of God for us hath bled; \\
        The Father's co-eternal Son \\
        Had all our sins upon Him laid. \\
        The Son of God for us hath died, \\
        Our Lord, our Life was crucified.

    \item Was crucified for us in shame \\
        To bring us rebels back to God. \\
        So may we glory in His name, \\
        As those redeemed by precious blood. \\
        Pardon and life flowed from His side, \\
        When He our Lord was crucified.

    \item Then let us glory in the cross, \\
        Make Christ our boast, our constant theme; \\
        All things for Him account but loss, \\
        And now for Him despise the shame. \\
        Let nought with Him our hearts divide \\
        Since He for us was crucified.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 17*}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. HUSHED WAS THE EVENING HYMN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HIMSELF He could not save, \\
        He on the cross must die, \\
        Or mercy could not come \\
        To ruined sinners nigh. \\
        Yes, Christ the Son of God must bleed, \\
        That sinners might from sin be freed.

    \item Himself He could not save, \\
        For justice must be done, \\
        Our sins' full weight must fall \\
        Upon the sinless One. \\
        For nothing less could God accept \\
        In payment of that fearful debt.

    \item Himself He could not save, \\
        Love's stream too deeply flowed, \\
        In love Himself He gave \\
        To pay the debt we owed. \\
        Obedience to the Father's will \\
        And love to Him did all fulfil.

    \item Himself He could not save, \\
        But now exalted high, \\
        A Prince and Saviour He \\
        Has saved and brought us nigh. \\
        We live in Him who lives above, \\
        And sing with joy His deathless love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 18*\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. EWING}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE Author of salvation \\
        Was offered on the tree. \\
        His deep humiliation \\
        In symbol here we see. \\
        We, by the Spirit guided, \\
        To Golgotha repair; \\
        The Lamb that God provided \\
        Was slaughtered for us there.

    \item The sword of God was bidden \\
        His Holy One to smite, \\
        Jehovah's face was hidden \\
        In terrors from His sight. \\
        God's tokens had declared Him \\
        His Son who pleased Him well; \\
        He pierced His soul, nor spared Him \\
        When bruised by earth and hell.

    \item Justice, our guilt to cover, \\
        Awoke the wrathful storm; \\
        Dismay seized friend and lover, \\
        He said, ``I am a worm.'' \\
        He was of God forsaken, \\
        As One by God abhorred, \\
        The sinner's place was taken \\
        By Him our glorious Lord.

    \item His God who duly prized Him, \\
        Whose statutes He did keep, \\
        In floods of death baptized Him, \\
        In sorrow's lowest deep. \\
        He was the Father's treasure, \\
        The Christ whom He had sent; \\
        His righteous sore displeasure \\
        On Him for us was spent.

    \item We on to ruin hurried, \\
        To misery's abyss, \\
        But dead with Him and buried, \\
        And raised to share His bliss, \\
        We sing with hearts united \\
        His cross for evermore; \\
        Once like the world benighted, \\
        His name we now adore. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 19*}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. TRICHINOPOLY}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE head once full of bruises, \\
        So full of pain and scorn, \\
        Midst other sore abuses \\
        Mocked with a crown of thorn, \\
        That head is now surrounded \\
        With brightest majesty; \\
        In death once bowed and wounded, \\
        Accurs\`ed on the tree.

    \item That countenance transcendent, \\
        That life-creating Sun \\
        To worlds on Him dependent, \\
        Was bruised and spit upon! \\
        That which His soul tormented \\
        Was our sins' heavy load, \\
        We had the debt augmented \\
        Which He then paid in blood.

    \item And oh! what consolation \\
        Doth in our hearts take place, \\
        When we His toil and passion \\
        Can joyfully retrace. \\
        Ah! should we while thus musing \\
        On our Redeemer's cross, \\
        E'en life itself be losing, \\
        Great gain would be that loss.

    \item We give Thee thanks unfeign\`ed \\
        For Him the Friend in need, \\
        For what His soul sustain\`ed \\
        When He for us did bleed. \\
        And now we rest unshaken \\
        Upon His faithfulness, \\
        Until we hence are taken \\
        To see Him face to face.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 20*\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. MUNICH}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item No bone of Him was broken--- \\
        The spotless paschal Lamb; \\
        Of life and peace a token \\
        To us who know His name. \\
        The Head for all the members \\
        The curse, the vengeance bore, \\
        And God, our God, remembers \\
        His people's sins no more.

    \item We, the redeemed, are reaping \\
        What Christ did sow in tears, \\
        The feast which we are keeping \\
        His name to us endears. \\
        It tells of Justice hiding \\
        From Him the face of God, \\
        Proud men around deriding \\
        His sorrow's awful load.

    \item His death of shame and sorrow \\
        Was like unto His birth, \\
        Which would no glory borrow, \\
        No majesty from earth. \\
        As pilgrims we are hasting \\
        To our eternal home, \\
        Its joy already tasting \\
        Of victory o'er the tomb.

    \item His life and death reviewing, \\
        We tread the narrow way; \\
        Our homeward path pursuing, \\
        We watch the dawn of day. \\
        We eat and drink with gladness \\
        The broken bread and wine, \\
        And sing with sweetest sadness \\
        Our song of love divine.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 21*}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. DUNSTAN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item CROWNED with thorns upon the tree, \\
        Silent in His agony, \\
        Dying crushed beneath the load \\
        Of the wrath and curse of God.

    \item On His pale and suffering brow, \\
        Myst\`ery of love and woe; \\
        On His grief and sore amaze \\
        We would fix our earnest gaze.

    \item Sin-atoning sacrifice, \\
        Oh how precious in our eyes! \\
        He alone our rest shall be, \\
        Now and through eternity.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 22*}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. MAINZER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SWEET is the savour of His name \\
        Who suffered in His people's stead; \\
        His portion here reproach and shame, \\
        He liveth now, He once was dead.

    \item He once was dead, the very same \\
        Who sits on yonder throne above, \\
        Who bears in heaven the greatest name, \\
        Whom angels serve, whom angels love.

    \item He once was dead, the very same \\
        Who made the worlds, a work of power, \\
        Who now upholds the mighty frame, \\
        And keeps it till the final hour.

    \item He once was dead; but now He lives, \\
        His glory fills all heaven above; \\
        Its blessedness to heaven He gives, \\
        The fountain He of joy and love.

    \item His people shall His triumph share, \\
        With Him shall live and with Him reign, \\
        In heaven their joy is full, for there \\
        They see Him who for them was slain.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 23*}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. INNOCENTS}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item LET us join our God to bless \\
        For His Son our righteousness, \\
        Him who bowed His head so low \\
        Underneath our load of woe.

    \item His the curse, the wounds, the gall, \\
        His the stripes, He bore them all; \\
        His the dying cry of pain \\
        When our sins He did sustain.

    \item He, the accepted sacrifice, \\
        From the vanquished grave did rise. \\
        Free Himself, He set us free \\
        In His perfect liberty.

    \item Praise our God who willed it thus, \\
        Praise His Son who died for us, \\
        Praise the Father for the Son \\
        Who so vast a work hath done.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 24}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. LUCERNE}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BRIGHTNESS of th' eternal glory--- \\
        Shall His praise unuttered lie? \\
        Who would hush the heaven-sent story \\
        Of the One who came to die?

    \item Came from Godhead's fullest glory \\
        Down to Calvary's depth of woe; \\
        Now on high, we bow before Him, \\
        Streams of praises ceaseless flow.

    \item Sing His blest triumphant rising, \\
        He is on the Father's throne; \\
        Sing till heaven and earth surprising \\
        He shall reign and He alone.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 25*\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. D. VESPER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SPOTLESS Lamb by God provided! \\
        Here upon His face we gaze, \\
        Where the Father's love and glory \\
        Shine in all their brightest rays. \\
        His almighty power and wisdom \\
        All creation's works proclaim; \\
        Heaven and earth alike confess Him \\
        As the ever great I AM.

    \item Lamb of God, His Father's bosom \\
        Ever was His dwelling-place; \\
        His delight, in Him rejoicing, \\
        One with Him in power and grace. \\
        Oh, what wondrous love and mercy \\
        Thus to lay His glory by, \\
        And for us to come from heav\`en \\
        As the Lamb of God to die!

    \item Lamb of God, when we behold Him \\
        Lowly in the manger laid, \\
        Wandering as a homeless Stranger \\
        In the world His hands had made, \\
        When we see Him in the garden, \\
        Anguish forcing drops like blood, \\
        At His grace we are confounded, \\
        Holy, spotless Lamb of God!

    \item When we see Him as the Victim \\
        Bound upon Golgotha's cross, \\
        For our guilt and folly stricken, \\
        Bearing judgment due to us, \\
        Lord, we own, with hearts adoring, \\
        He has loved us unto blood; \\
        Glory, glory everlasting \\
        Be unto the Lamb of God!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 26}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.8. MAN OF SORROWS}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item MAN of Sorrows! what a name \\
        For the Son of God, who came \\
        Ruined sinners to reclaim. \\
        Hallelujah! what a Saviour!

    \item Bearing shame and scoffing rude, \\
        In my place condemned He stood, \\
        Sealed my pardon with His blood. \\
        Hallelujah! what a Saviour!

    \item Guilty, vile, and helpless we, \\
        Spotless Lamb of God was He; \\
        Full atonement can it be? \\
        Hallelujah! what a Saviour!

    \item Lifted up was He to die, \\
        ``It is finished,'' was His cry, \\
        Now in heaven exalted high, \\
        Hallelujah! what a Saviour!

    \item When He comes, the glorious King, \\
        All His ransomed home to bring, \\
        Then anew this song we'll sing, \\
        Hallelujah! what a Saviour!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 27*}}
    \\
    \textit{C. M. NAOMI}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE love of God, it melts our hearts, \\
        Like ice before the sun; \\
        And fills our eyes with grateful tears, \\
        Such wonders it has done.

    \item When Justice stern in wrath arose \\
        With sword uplift and keen, \\
        Poor helpless sinners to dispatch, \\
        God's love did intervene.

    \item Between that mighty sword and us \\
        Came God's beloved Son. \\
        That fearful stroke, it fell on Him, \\
        And life for us was won.

    \item God's love to us will never cease, \\
        Unlimited it flows; \\
        As from an everlasting spring \\
        It faileth not, but grows.

    \item Then unto God be glory given, \\
        And let us praise Him now \\
        For wondrous love through Christ displayed, \\
        While at His throne we bow.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 28}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4. GOD IS LOVE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [The last line of each verse to be repeated]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GOD is love, His word has said it, \\
        This is news of heavenly birth; \\
        In our hearts His Spirit shed it, \\
        Made us know it here on earth, \\
        That God is love.

    \item Not in yonder bless\`ed regions \\
        Where the Lord, in glory crowned, \\
        Reigns amid angelic legions, \\
        Will the brightest proof be found \\
        That God is love.

    \item 'Tis on earth the Lord discloses \\
        All His love how vast it is; \\
        Earth's the favoured spot He chooses \\
        To display the truth of this--- \\
        That God is love.

    \item 'Tis that Man of Sorrows yonder, \\
        Object of contempt beneath, \\
        But in heaven of highest wonder, \\
        Teaches fully by His death \\
        That God is love.

    \item His a throne, the throne of heav\`en, \\
        Yet He comes to earth to bleed, \\
        And for man His life is giv\`en, \\
        This is what declares indeed \\
        That God is love.

    \item Not for those who ever loved Him \\
        Did the Lord of glory die; \\
        Pity to the wretched moved Him, \\
        Who that hears it will deny \\
        That God is love?

    \item Let us lowly now adore Him, \\
        Triune God of love and grace; \\
        Joyful bow the knee before Him, \\
        Laud His goodness, sound His praise, \\
        For God is love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 29*}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. DUKE STREET}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O BLESSED God, our voice we raise \\
        To speak Thy worth and sing Thy praise, \\
        For love that made Thee give Thy Son \\
        For sinners ruined and undone.

    \item On Him by Thee our sins were laid, \\
        When He for sin atonement made; \\
        Our every sin was known to Thee, \\
        He bore them that we might be free.

    \item Such love we cannot comprehend, \\
        That Thou for us Thy Son shouldst send \\
        To bear such heavy load of woe, \\
        To pay the debt that we did owe.

    \item But this we know, Thy Son did die \\
        To bring us guilty sinners nigh. \\
        Now all the work is fully done, \\
        We stand accepted in Thy Son.

    \item And now we wait the happy day \\
        When He will take His own away, \\
        Far from this scene of care and strife, \\
        To dwell with Him in endless life.

    \item Till then, O God, be this our aim, \\
        To celebrate His wondrous name, \\
        To praise the love that made Thee give \\
        Thy Son to die that we might live.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 30}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.6.8.8.6. MERIBAH}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD, to Thee our voice we lift \\
        In praise for every perfect gift \\
        Thou sendest from above. \\
        In daily boons bestowed by Thee \\
        Thou hast declared Thyself to be \\
        A God of grace and love.

    \item But oh, Thy Son, from heav\`en sent, \\
        Has love's unlimited extent, \\
        Its shoreless ocean, shown. \\
        For Him, our songs to Thee we raise \\
        In thankful gratitude and praise, \\
        The gift that makes Thee known.

    \item What love it was, that Thou shouldst give \\
        Thy Son to death that we might live, \\
        Who bore no love to Thee! \\
        No thanks we gave for aught bestowed, \\
        But by our every action showed \\
        What enemies were we.

    \item But now we love, for love expressed \\
        In giving us Thy very best--- \\
        Thy well-belov\`ed Son. \\
        We praise Thee, worship and adore, \\
        Who opened thus love's boundless store \\
        And gave that bless\`ed One.

    \item Oh, wondrous gift, in whom we find \\
        Thine every heavenly gift combined; \\
        Our all-in-all is He. \\
        Our life, our peace, our righteousness; \\
        All that we have and shall possess, \\
        We have in Him, from Thee.

    \item To Thee is highest glory due, \\
        And ages shall its weight accrue, \\
        For this that Thou hast done. \\
        So now we'd join that mighty song \\
        That age on age shall echo on, \\
        Praise for Thy gift---Thy Son.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 31*}}
    \\
    \textit{6.10.10.6. NEW HEREFORD}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BLESSED be God, our God, \\
        Who gave for us His well-belov\`ed Son, \\
        The gift of gifts, all other gifts in one; \\
        Bless\`ed be God, our God!

    \item What will He not bestow, \\
        Who gave for us this mighty gift unbought, \\
        Unmerited, unheeded, and unsought? \\
        What will He not bestow?

    \item He spar\`ed not His Son! \\
        'Tis this that silences each rising fear, \\
        'Tis this that bids the hard thought disappear; \\
        He spar\`ed not His Son!

    \item Who shall condemn us now, \\
        Since Christ has died and risen and gone above, \\
        For us to plead at the right hand of Love? \\
        Who shall condemn us now?

    \item 'Tis God that justifies! \\
        Who shall recall His pardon or His grace? \\
        Or who the broken chain of guilt replace? \\
        'Tis God that justifies!

    \item The vict\`ory is ours! \\
        For us in might came forth the Mighty One; \\
        For us He fought the fight, the triumph won; \\
        The vict\`ory is ours!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 32*}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.4.6.6.6.4. OLIVET}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GLORY to God on high, \\
        Peace upon earth and joy, \\
        Goodwill to man. \\
        We who God's blessing prove \\
        (His name all names above), \\
        Sing now redeeming love, \\
        Too vast to scan.

    \item Mercy and Truth unite, \\
        Oh, 'tis a wondrous sight, \\
        All sights above. \\
        Jesus the curse sustains, \\
        Guilt's bitter cup He drains, \\
        Nothing for us remains, \\
        Nothing but love.

    \item Love that no tongue can teach, \\
        Love that no thought can reach, \\
        No love like His. \\
        God is its bless\`ed source, \\
        Death ne'er can stop its course, \\
        Nothing can stay its force, \\
        Matchless it is.

    \item Blest in this love we sing, \\
        To God our praises bring, \\
        All sins forgiven. \\
        Honour and majesty \\
        Now and for ever be \\
        To the blest Trinity, \\
        Here and in heaven. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 33}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.4.6.6.6.4. MOSCOW}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GLORY to God on high! \\
        Let heaven and earth reply, \\
        Praise ye His name. \\
        His love and grace adore, \\
        Who all our sorrows bore; \\
        Sing now and evermore--- \\
        Worthy His name.

    \item Let us, a ransomed race, \\
        Sing His abounding grace, \\
        Praise to His name. \\
        Tell what His arm hath done, \\
        What spoils from Death He won, \\
        God's well-belov\`ed Son, \\
        Worthy His name.

    \item Jesus, the Son of God, \\
        Bore sin's accurs\`ed load, \\
        Praise to His name. \\
        Sing, we who know His blood \\
        Hath brought us nigh to God, \\
        And crowned us with all good, \\
        Worthy His name.

    \item Let all the hosts above \\
        Join in one song of love, \\
        Praise to His name. \\
        To Him ascrib\`ed be \\
        Honour and majesty, \\
        Through all eternity, \\
        Worthy His name.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 34}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.4.6.6.6.4. THERESA}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD, how good art Thou! \\
        Before Thy throne we bow, \\
        We praise Thy name. \\
        Thou art the only wise, \\
        Thy throne above the skies, \\
        Thy glory never dies; \\
        We praise Thy name.

    \item Oh, what a gift Thy Son! \\
        He all Thy will has done, \\
        His name we praise. \\
        He bore the penalty, \\
        He suffered on the tree, \\
        He set the captives free; \\
        His name we praise.

    \item The Paraclete came down, \\
        The gracious work to crown, \\
        His name we praise. \\
        He, sent in Jesus' name, \\
        Down from the glory came, \\
        Our souls with love to 'nflame; \\
        His name we praise.

    \item Thou bless\`ed Trinity, \\
        We lowly worship Thee, \\
        We praise Thy name. \\
        Taught by the heavenly Dove, \\
        We lift our hearts above, \\
        We sing Thy power and love, \\
        We praise Thy name.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 35*}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. HARTS}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GLORY to the Holy Three, \\
        Father, Son and Spirit be; \\
        Him who gave, and Him who died, \\
        Him who with us doth abide.

    \item Glory be to Him who gave \\
        His own Son our souls to save, \\
        Sent Him down from heaven above \\
        To reveal His Father's love.

    \item Glory to th' incarnate Word, \\
        Our all-worthy Kinsman, Lord; \\
        Lord of earth and Lord of heaven, \\
        Who for us His life hath given.

    \item Glory to the Spirit pure, \\
        He doth to our hearts make sure \\
        All the goodness of our God, \\
        All the worth of Jesus' blood.

    \item Love of Father, love of Son, \\
        Love of aye-abiding One; \\
        Glory, honour, worship be \\
        To the undivided Three. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 36*}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. ST. JOHN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WHAT was it, O our God, \\
        Led Thee to give Thy Son, \\
        To yield Thy Well-beloved \\
        For us by sin undone? \\
        'Twas love unbounded led Thee thus \\
        To give Thy Well-beloved for us.

    \item What led the Son of God \\
        To leave His throne on high, \\
        To shed His precious blood, \\
        To suffer and to die? \\
        'Twas love, unbounded love to us, \\
        Led Him to die and suffer thus.

    \item What moved Thee to impart \\
        Thy Spirit from above, \\
        Therewith to fill each heart \\
        With heavenly peace and love? \\
        'Twas love, unbounded love to us, \\
        Moved Thee to give Thy Spirit thus.

    \item What doth the Spirit move, \\
        Within us to abide, \\
        To teach us all Thy love, \\
        And tell of Him who died? \\
        'Tis love unbounded moves Him thus \\
        To condescend to dwell with us.

    \item What love to Thee we owe, \\
        O God, for all Thy grace! \\
        Our hearts should overflow \\
        In everlasting praise. \\
        Help us, O God, to praise Thee thus \\
        For all Thy boundless love to us.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 37}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. LENOX}
    \\
    \textit{\small [The last line of each verse to be repeated]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE give eternal praise \\
        To God the Father's love, \\
        For all our comforts here \\
        And better hopes above; \\
        He sent His own eternal Son \\
        To die for sins that we had done.

    \item To God the Son belongs \\
        Eternal glory too, \\
        Who bought us with His blood \\
        From everlasting woe; \\
        And now He lives and soon will reign \\
        To see the fruit of all His pain.

    \item To God the Spirit's name \\
        Eternal thanks we give, \\
        Whose new-creating power \\
        Makes the dead sinner live; \\
        His work completes the great design \\
        And fills the soul with joy divine.

    \item Almighty God, to Thee \\
        Be endless honours done, \\
        The undivided Three \\
        And self-existent One; \\
        Where reason fails with all her powers, \\
        There faith prevails and love adores.

\end{enumerate}
\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 2}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{The Lord's Resurrection and Triumph}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 38}}
    \\
    \textit{6.5.6.4. LOWRY}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item Low in the grave He lay, \\
        Jesus, my Saviour! \\
        Waiting the coming day, \\
        Jesus, my Lord! \\
        Up from the grave He arose \\
        With a mighty triumph o'er His foes; \\
        He arose a Victor from the dark domain, \\
        And He lives for ever with His saints to reign; \\
        He arose! \\
        Hallelujah! Christ arose!

    \item Vainly they watch His bed, \\
        Jesus, my Saviour! \\
        Vainly they seal the dead, \\
        Jesus, my Lord!

    \item Death cannot keep his prey, \\
        Jesus, my Saviour! \\
        He tore the bars away! \\
        Jesus, my Lord!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 39}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. EASTER HYMN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item CHRIST the Lord is risen to-day, Hallelujah! \\
        Sons of men and angels say, Hallelujah! \\
        Raise your joys and triumphs high, Hallelujah! \\
        Sing, ye heavens, and earth reply, Hallelujah!

    \item Love's redeeming work is done, Hallelujah! \\
        Fought the fight, the battle won, Hallelujah! \\
        Lo, our Sun's eclipse is o'er, Hallelujah! \\
        Lo, He sets in death no more, Hallelujah!

    \item Vain the stone, the watch, the seal, Hallelujah! \\
        Christ hath burst the gates of Sheol, Hallelujah! \\
        Death in vain forbids Him rise, Hallelujah! \\
        Christ hath opened Paradise, Hallelujah!

    \item Lives again the glorious King, Hallelujah! \\
        Where, O Death, is now thy sting? Hallelujah! \\
        Once He died our souls to save, Hallelujah! \\
        Where's thy vict\`ory, O Grave? Hallelujah!

    \item Lord of earth and heaven He is, Hallelujah! \\
        Everlasting life is this, Hallelujah! \\
        Him to know, His power to prove, Hallelujah! \\
        Him to laud, and Him to love, Hallelujah! Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 40}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. GWALCHMAI}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HAIL the day that sees Him rise, Hallelujah! \\
        To the throne above the skies, Hallelujah! \\
        Christ, whose life for us was given, Hallelujah! \\
        Enters now the highest heaven, Hallelujah!

    \item There for Him high triumph waits, Hallelujah! \\
        Lift your heads, eternal gates, Hallelujah! \\
        He has conquered death and sin, Hallelujah! \\
        Let the King of glory in, Hallelujah!

    \item See, He lifts His hands above, Hallelujah! \\
        See, He shows the prints of love, Hallelujah! \\
        Hark, His gracious lips bestow, Hallelujah! \\
        Blessings on His saints below, Hallelujah!

    \item There for us He intercedes, Hallelujah! \\
        His atoning death He pleads, Hallelujah! \\
        Near Himself prepares our place, Hallelujah! \\
        In the riches of His grace, Hallelujah!

    \item Now He's parted from our sight, Hallelujah! \\
        Far above the starry height, Hallelujah! \\
        Soon for us He'll come again, Hallelujah! \\
        Christ, who for our sins was slain, Hallelujah!

    \item He shall come and take us home, Hallelujah! \\
        From Him we no more shall roam, Hallelujah! \\
        Sing His glory, hymn His praise, Hallelujah! \\
        Joyful through eternal days, Hallelujah!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 41}}
    \\
    \textit{D. C. M. with Chorus ST. GEORGE'S, EDINBURGH}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Lines 7 & 8 to be repeated. To be sung at the end of the hymn.]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item YE gates, lift up your heads on high; \\
        Ye doors that last for aye, \\
        Be lifted up, that so the King \\
        Of glory enter may! \\
        But who of glory is the King? \\
        The mighty Lord is this, \\
        E'en that same Lord that great in might \\
        And strong in battle is.

    \item Ye gates, lift up your heads; ye doors, \\
        Doors that do last for aye, \\
        Be lifted up, that so the King \\
        Of glory enter may! \\
        But who is He that is the King \\
        Of glory? Who is this? \\
        The Lord of hosts, and none but He, \\
        The King of glory is. \\
        Hallelujah! Hallelujah! \\
        Praise Jehovah! Bless Jehovah! \\
        Hallelujah! Amen, amen, amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 42}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. DARWALL'S 148TH}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE happy morn is come; \\
        Triumphant o'er the grave \\
        The Saviour leaves the tomb, \\
        Almighty now to save. \\
        Captivity is captive led, \\
        Since Jesus liveth, who was dead.

    \item Who now accuseth them \\
        For whom the Surety died? \\
        Or who shall those condemn \\
        Whom God hath justified? \\
        Captivity is captive led, \\
        Since Jesus liveth, who was dead.

    \item Christ hath the ransom paid, \\
        The glorious work is done; \\
        On Him our help was laid, \\
        The vict\`ory is won. \\
        Captivity is captive led, \\
        Since Jesus liveth, who was dead.

    \item To the triumphant Lord \\
        (The Resurrection He), \\
        To the incarnate Word \\
        Eternal honours be. \\
        Captivity is captive led, \\
        Since Jesus liveth, who was dead.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 43}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.6.8.8.6. PRAISE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [The last line of each verse to be repeated, and then the last three lines to be repeated.]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item OH joyful day, oh glorious hour, \\
        When Jesus by almighty power \\
        Arose and left the grave! \\
        In all His works behold Him great, \\
        Before, almighty to create, \\
        Almighty now to save.

    \item The First-begotten from the dead, \\
        He's risen now, His people's Head, \\
        And thus their life's secure. \\
        And if like Him they yield their breath, \\
        Like Him they'll burst the bonds of death, \\
        Their resurrection sure.

    \item Why should His people then be sad? \\
        None have such reason to be glad \\
        As those redeemed to God. \\
        Jesus the mighty Saviour lives, \\
        To them eternal life He gives, \\
        The purchase of His blood.

    \item Then let our gladsome praise resound, \\
        And let us in His work abound, \\
        Whose bless\`ed name is Love. \\
        We're sure our labour's not in vain, \\
        For we with Him ere long shall reign, \\
        With Jesus dwell above.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 44}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M. MOUNT EPHRAIM}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE Lord is risen indeed! \\
        Then Justice asks no more; \\
        Mercy and Truth are now agreed, \\
        Who stood opposed before.

    \item The Lord is risen indeed! \\
        Then is His work performed; \\
        The captive Surety now is freed, \\
        And death our foe disarmed.

    \item The Lord is risen indeed! \\
        He lives to die no more, \\
        He lives His people's cause to plead, \\
        Whose curse and shame He bore.

    \item The Lord is risen indeed! \\
        Hades has lost its prey; \\
        And with Him all the ransomed seed \\
        Shall reign in endless day.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 45}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M. ST. MICHAEL'S}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HIS be the Victor's name \\
        Who fought the fight alone; \\
        Triumphant saints no honour claim, \\
        Their conquest was His own.

    \item By weakness and defeat \\
        He won the meed and crown; \\
        Trod all our foes beneath His feet \\
        By being trodden down.

    \item He hell in hell laid low, \\
        Made sin, He sin o'erthrew, \\
        Bowed to the grave, destroyed it so, \\
        And death by dying slew.

    \item Bless, bless the Conqueror slain, \\
        Slain in His vict\`ory, \\
        Who lived, who died, who lives again \\
        For thee, His Church, for thee.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 46}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. ST. MAGNUS}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BEHOLD the Lord with glory crowned, \\
        To Him all power is given; \\
        No place too high for Him is found, \\
        No place too high in heaven.

    \item He fills the throne, the throne above, \\
        He fills it without wrong; \\
        The object of His Father's love, \\
        The theme of angels' song.

    \item Though high yet He presents the praise \\
        God's people offer here; \\
        The faintest, feeblest lay they raise \\
        Will reach the Father's ear.

    \item This song be ours, and this alone \\
        That celebrates the fame \\
        Of Him who sits upon the throne, \\
        And that exalts His name.

    \item To Him whom men despise and slight, \\
        To Him be glory given. \\
        The crown is His, and His by right \\
        The highest place in heaven.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 47}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. OLD WINCHESTER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE head that once was crowned with thorns \\
        Is crowned with glory now, \\
        A royal diadem adorns \\
        The mighty Victor's brow.

    \item Delight of all who dwell above, \\
        The joy of saints below, \\
        To us He manifests His love \\
        That we its depths may know.

    \item To us His cross with all its shame, \\
        With all its grace is given; \\
        Though earth disowns the lowly name, \\
        All worship it in heaven.

    \item To us His cross is life and health, \\
        Though shame and death to Him; \\
        Our present glory, joy, and wealth, \\
        Our never-ending theme.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 48}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. SOUTHWELL}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE Son of God, who dwelt in light \\
        Unreached by mortal eye, \\
        Came forth as man the foe to fight, \\
        And won the vict\`ory.

    \item In perfect light was sin made bare, \\
        It met its utmost due; \\
        While perfect love in triumph there \\
        Revealed salvation too.

    \item Who but the sinless One could be \\
        Sin-offering meet for God? \\
        And who in heaven or earth save He \\
        Could cleanse us with His blood?

    \item Exalted now at God's right hand \\
        He sits enthroned above; \\
        Eternal proof that God is just, \\
        And proof that God is love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 49}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. DUNFERMLINE}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item For whom is yonder crown prepared, \\
        Of workmanship divine? \\
        For Jesus is the bright reward, \\
        For Him its glories shine.

    \item Beneath the earth awhile He lies, \\
        A Prisoner with the dead. \\
        A Victor soon the Lord will rise, \\
        And glory wreathe His head.

    \item He saw the cross, despised its shame, \\
        And bowed beneath its weight. \\
        For this He bears the greatest name, \\
        And gains the highest seat.

    \item To Him shall every knee be bowed, \\
        His claim shall angels own, \\
        Around the rising Victor crowd \\
        And bear Him to the throne.

    \item We think we see the glorious King \\
        By hosts angelic owned; \\
        They shout and heaven's high arches ring \\
        With the triumphant sound.

    \item Let saints on earth their tribute bring, \\
        And echo back the sound, \\
        For He who saves them is the King \\
        By hosts angelic owned.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 50}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. STUTTGART}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HARK, ten thousand voices crying, \\
        "Lamb of God," with one accord! \\
        Thousand, thousand saints replying \\
        Wake at once the echoing chord.

    \item Praise the Lamb! the chorus waking, \\
        All in heaven together throng; \\
        Loud and far, each tongue partaking, \\
        Rolls along the endless song.

    \item Grateful incense this ascending \\
        Ever to the Father's throne; \\
        Every knee to Jesus bending, \\
        All the mind of heaven is one.

    \item All the Father's counsels claiming \\
        Equal honours to the Son; \\
        All the Son's effulgence beaming \\
        Makes the Father's glory known.

    \item By the Spirit all pervading, \\
        Hosts unnumbered round the Lamb \\
        Crowd with light and joy unfading, \\
        Hail Him as the great I AM.

    \item Joyful now the wide creation \\
        Rests in undisturbed repose, \\
        Blest in Jesus' full salvation \\
        Sorrow now nor thraldom knows.

    \item Hark! the heavenly notes of praises--- \\
        Loudly swells the song again; \\
        Joy responsive "Amen!" raises, \\
        Hallelujah, and Amen!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 51}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. SHERBORNE}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item KING of kings and Lord of lords--- \\
        These great awe-inspiring words \\
        Unto Christ the Lord belong; \\
        Let His people raise their song.

    \item Glory, honour, praise and power, \\
        These are His for evermore. \\
        He is on the throne above, \\
        Jesus, who told out God's love.

    \item Holy, holy, holy Lord, \\
        Heaven and earth His name record. \\
        Power and praise to Him belong, \\
        "He is worthy," is our song.

    \item Rich in glory, Christ did stoop, \\
        Thence is all His people's hope. \\
        He was poor that we might be \\
        Rich with Him eternally.

    \item When we think of love like this, \\
        Joy and shame our hearts possess; \\
        Joy that He could pity thus, \\
        Shame for such return from us.

    \item Yet we soon the day shall see \\
        When we shall from sin be free, \\
        Borne aloft, to heav\`en brought, \\
        There to praise God as we ought.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 52}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8. VICTORY}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item Hallelujah! \\
        The strife is o'er, the battle done, \\
        The triumph of Jehovah won; \\
        Oh, let the song of praise be sung, \\
        Hallelujah!

    \item The powers of death have done their worst, \\
        Christ Jesus hath His foes dispersed; \\
        Let shouts of praise and joy outburst, \\
        Hallelujah!

    \item On that third day He rose again \\
        In glorious majesty to reign; \\
        Oh, let us swell the joyful strain, \\
        Hallelujah!

    \item He overcame the power of hell, \\
        The bars from heaven's high portals fell; \\
        Let songs of joy His triumph tell, \\
        Hallelujah!

    \item He died upon Golgotha's tree, \\
        From death's dread sting to set us free, \\
        That we, O God, may sing to Thee \\
        Hallelujah! Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 53}}
    \\
    \textit{Irregular RESURRECTION}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Verse 1: Fast; Verses 2-5: Slower; Verse 6: Quicker]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HARK, ten thousand voices cry, \\
        Vict\`ory! through the sky. \\
        Swiftly flies the welcome sound, \\
        Spreading rapturous joy around.

    \item Jesus comes, His conflict over, \\
        Comes to claim His great reward; \\
        Angels round the Victor hover, \\
        Crowding to behold their Lord.

    \item Oh, what honours now await Him! \\
        Friends and foes shall hear His voice; \\
        Tremble, tremble, ye that hate Him, \\
        Ye who love His name rejoice.

    \item Yonder throne for Him erected \\
        Now becomes the Victor's seat, \\
        Lo, the Man on earth rejected, \\
        Angels worship at His feet!

    \item Day and night they cry before Him, \\
        Holy, holy, holy Lord. \\
        All the powers of heaven adore Him, \\
        All obey His sovereign word.

    \item Then haste, ye saints, your tribute bring \\
        And laud the everlasting King; \\
        Laud Him, \\
        Laud the everlasting King.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 54}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.4.7. MIGDOL}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item LOOK, ye saints, the sight is glorious, \\
        See the Man of Sorrows now! \\
        From the fight returned victorious, \\
        Every knee to Him shall bow. \\
        God has crowned Him, \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        Crowns become the Victor's brow.

    \item See the Saviour God has crown\`ed! \\
        Rich the trophies that He brings; \\
        In the seat of power enthron\`ed, \\
        While the vault of heav\`en rings. \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        God has crowned Him, \\
        Crowned the Saviour King of kings.

    \item Sinners in derision crowned Him, \\
        Mocking thus the Saviour's claim; \\
        Saints and angels crowd around Him, \\
        Own His title, praise His name. \\
        God has crowned Him, \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        Sing aloud the Victor's fame.

    \item Hark! those bursts of acclamation, \\
        Hark! those loud triumphant chords; \\
        Jesus takes the highest station, \\
        Oh, what joy the sight affords! \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        God has crowned Him, \\
        King of kings and Lord of lords.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 55}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.7. DISMISSION}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item On the Father's throne is seated \\
        Christ the Lord, the Living One; \\
        All His toil on earth completed, \\
        All His work for sinners done. \\
        In the glory \\
        See Him, God's eternal Son.

    \item Every knee shall bow before Him, \\
        Every tongue confess His name. \\
        Ransomed myriads shall adore Him \\
        Who endured the cross and shame. \\
        From the glory \\
        God doth now His worth proclaim.

    \item Man to Him the cross awarded, \\
        Man the Saviour crucified; \\
        This world's judgment stands recorded, \\
        God's own nature glorified. \\
        By the glory \\
        Christ was claimed on earth who died.

    \item Son of Man, His incarnation \\
        Opened first the tale of grace. \\
        Son of Man, in new creation \\
        Leader of a chosen race. \\
        Well may glory \\
        Own Him in the highest place.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 56*}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. D. ST. GEORGE'S, WINDSOR}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item CROWNS of glory ever bright \\
        Rest upon the Victor's head. \\
        Crowns of glory are His right, \\
        His who liveth and was dead. \\
        Jesus fought and won the day, \\
        Such a day was never fought! \\
        Well His people now may say, \\
        See what God, our God, has wrought.

    \item He subdued the powers of hell; \\
        In the fight He stood alone; \\
        All His foes before Him fell, \\
        By His single arm o'erthrown. \\
        They have fallen to rise no more, \\
        Final is the foes' defeat; \\
        Jesus triumphed by His power, \\
        And His triumph is complete.

    \item His the fight, the arduous toil, \\
        His the honours of the day, \\
        His the glory and the spoil, \\
        Jesus bears them all away. \\
        Now exalt His peerless name, \\
        Sing aloud of His renown--- \\
        His the never-dying fame, \\
        His the well-deserv\`ed crown!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 57*}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.8.8.7. LUTHER'S HYMN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE Lord of glory! Who is He? \\
        Who is the King of glory? \\
        Only the Son of God can be \\
        The Christ, the King of glory. \\
        Consider all His wounds, and see \\
        How Jesus' death upon the tree \\
        Proclaims Him King of glory.

    \item Above all heavens at God's right hand \\
        Now sits the King of glory. \\
        The angels by His favour stand \\
        Before the throne of glory; \\
        Swiftly they fly at His command \\
        To guard His own of every land, \\
        To keep the heirs of glory.

    \item Death and the grave confess the Lamb \\
        To be the King of glory; \\
        The powers of darkness dread His name, \\
        Creation shows His glory. \\
        He said, "Ere Abra'am was I am," \\
        Jesus is evermore the same--- \\
        Th' Almighty King of glory.

    \item Thrice happy who in Him believe, \\
        They soon shall share His glory; \\
        Born of His Spirit, they receive \\
        His secret pledge of glory. \\
        Taught by His cross for sin they grieve; \\
        He calls them brethren, and they cleave \\
        To Him, their hope of glory.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 58}}
    \\
    \textit{Irreg. EGYPT}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE mighty work is done; \\
        He who the victory won \\
        No more will die; He lives on high, \\
        His triumph loud we sing. \\
        Our praise to God we bring--- \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        God has raised Him from the dead; \\
        Now He lives our glorious Head.

    \item He entered heaven by blood; \\
        Upon the throne of God \\
        He sitteth now; to Him all bow; \\
        His priceless worth we sing, \\
        Our praise to God we bring--- \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        God has seated Him on high, \\
        And by Him we are brought nigh.

    \item He comes soon to the air, \\
        And we shall meet Him there, \\
        Oh glorious hour of heavenly power! \\
        Then gladsome songs we'll sing, \\
        And heaven's high vault shall ring. \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        We shall know His love and grace \\
        When we look upon His face.

    \item He comes to earth to reign, \\
        The Lamb that once was slain, \\
        His bounteous hand in every land \\
        Proclaims Him heav\`en's King. \\
        All people then shall sing--- \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        Soon He comes to earth again \\
        As the King of kings to reign.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 59*}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. ST. PETER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE veil is rent! Lo, Jesus sits \\
        Upon a throne of grace; \\
        The incense which His name emits \\
        Fills all that glorious place.

    \item His precious blood is sprinkled there \\
        Before and on the throne, \\
        And His own wounds in heaven declare \\
        His work on earth is done.

    \item "'Tis finished," on the cross He said \\
        In agonies and blood; \\
        "'Tis finished," now He lives to plead \\
        Before the face of God.

    \item "'Tis finished," here our souls can rest, \\
        His work can never fail; \\
        By Him, our sacrifice and Priest, \\
        We enter through the veil.

    \item Within the Holiest of all, \\
        Cleansed by His precious blood, \\
        Before Thy throne Thy people fall \\
        And worship Thee, O God.

    \item Boldly our heart and voice we raise, \\
        His name, His blood, our plea, \\
        Assured our sacrifice of praise \\
        Ascends by Him to Thee.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 60*}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. HARRINGTON}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item 'Tis past, the dark and dreary night; \\
        O God, we see Him now, \\
        Our morning Star, without a cloud \\
        Of sadness on His brow.

    \item His path on earth, the cross, the grave, \\
        His sorrows, all are o'er; \\
        And oh, sweet thought! His eye shall weep, \\
        His heart shall break no more.

    \item Deep were those sorrows, deeper still \\
        The love that brought Him low, \\
        That bade the streams of life from Him \\
        A lifeless Victim flow.

    \item The soldier, as he pierced Him, proved \\
        Man's hate to Him and Thee, \\
        Whilst in the blood that stained the spear \\
        His love and Thine we see.

    \item It is not that we know the joy \\
        Of cancelled sin alone, \\
        But happier far, the saints are called \\
        To share His glorious throne.

    \item So closely are we linked in love \\
        (As dear as He to Thee) \\
        That all His bliss and glory soon \\
        Our portion blest shall be.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 61*}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. BONAR}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item DONE is the work that saves, \\
        Once and for ever done! \\
        Finished the righteousness \\
        That clothes th' unrighteous one! \\
        The love that blesses us below \\
        Is flowing freely to us now.

    \item The sacrifice is o'er, \\
        The veil is rent in twain, \\
        The mercy-seat is red \\
        With blood of Victim slain. \\
        Why stand we, then, without in fear? \\
        The blood of Christ invites us near.

    \item The gate is open wide, \\
        The new and living way \\
        Is clear and free and bright \\
        With love and peace and day. \\
        Into the Holies now we come--- \\
        Our present and eternal home.

    \item Enthroned in majesty \\
        The great Priest sits within. \\
        The precious blood once shed \\
        Has made and keeps us clean. \\
        With boldness let us now draw near, \\
        That blood has banished every fear.

    \item To Him who has been slain \\
        Be glory, praise and power; \\
        He died, and lives again, \\
        He lives for evermore. \\
        He loves us, cleansed us by His blood, \\
        Made us a kingdom, priests to God.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 62}}
    \\
    \textit{Irregular WESLEY}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HEAD of the Church triumphant, \\
        Now seated in the glory! \\
        Till He appear \\
        His members here, \\
        O God, would bow before Thee. \\
        Head of the Church triumphant! \\
        We lift our hearts and voices \\
        In blest anticipation, \\
        And cry aloud, \\
        And give to God \\
        The praise of our salvation.

    \item By faith we see the glory \\
        Of which Thou dost assure us, \\
        The world despise \\
        For that high prize \\
        Which Thou hast set before us. \\
        We wait with expectation \\
        The happy consummation \\
        Of His blest promise given, \\
        To meet our Lord \\
        By all adored, \\
        And swell the praise of heaven.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 63}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8. ST. AIDAN (GRAY)}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item FROM far we see the glorious day \\
        When He who bore our sins away \\
        Will all His majesty display.

    \item A Man of Sorrows once He was; \\
        No friend was found to plead His cause, \\
        For all preferred the world's applause.

    \item He groaned beneath sin's awful load, \\
        For in the sinner's place He stood, \\
        And died to bring us nigh to God.

    \item But now He reigns with glory crowned, \\
        While angel hosts the throne surround, \\
        And still His lofty praises sound.

    \item And there is yet a day to come \\
        When He who suffered in our room \\
        Will take His waiting people home.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 64}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. OLD 148TH}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item TH'ATONING work is done, \\
        The Victim's blood is shed, \\
        And Jesus now is gone \\
        His people's cause to plead. \\
        He lives in heaven, their great High Priest, \\
        And bears their names upon His breast.

    \item He sprinkled with His blood \\
        The mercy-seat above, \\
        For Justice had withstood \\
        The purposes of Love. \\
        But Justice now withstands no more \\
        And Mercy yields her boundless store.

    \item No temple made with hands \\
        His place of service is; \\
        In heaven itself He stands, \\
        A heavenly priesthood His; \\
        In Him the shadows of the law \\
        Are all fulfilled, and now withdraw.

    \item And though awhile He be \\
        Hid from the eyes of men, \\
        His people look to see \\
        Their great High Priest again. \\
        In brightest glory He will come \\
        And take His waiting people home.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 65*}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. DIJON}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item JESUS in the heavenly temple \\
        Sits with God upon the throne, \\
        Now no more to be forsaken, \\
        His humiliation gone.

    \item Never more shall God the Father \\
        Smite the Shepherd with the sword; \\
        Ne'er again shall lawless sinners \\
        Set at nought the glorious Lord.

    \item Dwelling in eternal sunshine \\
        Of the countenance of God, \\
        Jesus fills all heaven with incense \\
        Of His rich atoning blood.

    \item On His heart our names are graven, \\
        On His shoulders we are borne. \\
        For His sake the Father loves us; \\
        Praise becomes us in return.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 66*}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. MARINERS'}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item RISE, each soul, behold, 'tis Jesus! \\
        Jesus fills our wondering eyes; \\
        See Him now in glory seated \\
        Where our sins no more can rise.

    \item There in righteousness transcendent, \\
        Lo, He doth in heaven appear! \\
        Shows the blood of His atonement \\
        As our title to be there.

    \item All our sins were laid upon Him, \\
        Jesus bore them on the tree; \\
        God, who knew them, laid them on Him, \\
        And, believing, we are free.

    \item God now brings us to His dwelling, \\
        Spreads for us a feast of love, \\
        Bids us welcome, ever telling \\
        What our portion is above.

    \item In that circle of God's favour, \\
        Circle of the Father's love, \\
        All is rest, and rest for ever, \\
        All is perfectness above.

    \item Blessed, glorious word "for ever," \\
        Yes, "for ever" is the word; \\
        Nothing can the ransomed sever, \\
        Nought divide them from the Lord.

\end{enumerate}
\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 3}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{The Excellencies of the Lord Jesus Christ}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 67}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. ANWOTH}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item ERE God had built the mountains, \\
        Or raised the fruitful hills; \\
        Before He filled the fountains \\
        That feed the running rills; \\
        In One from everlasting, \\
        The wonderful I AM \\
        Found pleasures never wasting, \\
        And Wisdom was His name.

    \item When, like a tent to dwell in, \\
        He spread the skies abroad, \\
        And swathed about the swelling \\
        Of ocean's mighty flood, \\
        He wrought by weight and measure; \\
        Wisdom was with Him then; \\
        Himself the Father's pleasure, \\
        And His the sons of men.

    \item Thus Wisdom's words discover \\
        His glory and His grace, \\
        The everlasting Lover \\
        Of our unworthy race. \\
        His gracious eye surveyed us \\
        Ere stars were seen above; \\
        In wisdom He has made us, \\
        And died for us in love.

    \item The Lord is now delighted \\
        To have us for His own, \\
        The love no longer slighted \\
        Which He to us has shown; \\
        In spite of all our badness, \\
        Oh, miracle of grace! \\
        He brings us in with gladness \\
        Before His Father's face.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 68}}
    \\
    \textit{8.6.8.6.8.8. SUPREMACY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [The last line to be repeated]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE laud the everlasting Word, \\
        The Father's only Son, \\
        God manifestly seen and heard, \\
        And heaven's belov\`ed One. \\
        Worthy the name of Jesus now \\
        That every knee therein should bow.

    \item In Him, most perfectly expressed, \\
        The Father's glories shine; \\
        Of the full Deity possessed, \\
        Eternally divine. \\
        Worthy the name of Jesus now \\
        That every knee therein should bow.

    \item True image of the Infinite \\
        Whose essence is concealed; \\
        Brightness of uncreated light, \\
        The heart of God revealed. \\
        Worthy the name of Jesus now \\
        That every knee therein should bow.

    \item But the high mysteries of His name \\
        An angel's grasp transcend; \\
        The Father only (glorious claim) \\
        The Son can comprehend. \\
        Worthy the name of Jesus now \\
