        That every knee therein should bow.

    \item Yet loving Him, on whom God's love \\
        Ineffable doth rest, \\
        His members all---below, above--- \\
        As one with Him are blest. \\
        Worthy the name of Jesus now \\
        That every knee therein should bow.

    \item Of the vast universe of bliss \\
        The centre He and sun; \\
        Th' eternal theme of praise is this--- \\
        God's well-belov\`ed One. \\
        Worthy the name of Jesus now \\
        That every knee therein should bow.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 69}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M.D. CREATION.}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item COME, let us sing this song of love, \\
        Of Him who came from heaven above; \\
        He made the worlds by His great power, \\
        And keeps them till the final hour. \\
        Creation doth His word obey, \\
        And none can stay His hand or say \\
        "What doest Thou? Thy wonders cease." \\
        He is the God of power and peace.

    \item He rules through heaven and earth and hell, \\
        His creatures all His wonders tell; \\
        In His own hand He holds the seas, \\
        He guides the tide, controls the breeze; \\
        The frost, the snow, the ice, the cold, \\
        All are by His great power controlled; \\
        And yet an infant Babe He lay \\
        In Bethlehem's manger on that day.

    \item The One who dwells in heaven so high \\
        We see within the manger lie; \\
        Th' eternal God, a little Child, \\
        So gentle, quiet, meek and mild. \\
        He came His Father to obey, \\
        Our sins by death to put away; \\
        Came to redeem us and to save \\
        From sin, from death and from the grave.

    \item To God our joyful praise we bring; \\
        His love, His power to save we sing. \\
        Our hearts adore Thee now, O God; \\
        We trace with joy the path He trod. \\
        With heart and voice we sing this day, \\
        And humbly we our homage pay; \\
        We wait to see His glorious face, \\
        And evermore to sing Thy grace.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 70}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.7. PRAISE, MY SOUL}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GLORY, glory everlasting \\
        Be to Him who bare the cross, \\
        Who redeemed our souls by tasting \\
        Death, the death deserved by us! \\
        Spread His glory \\
        Who redeemed His people thus.

    \item His is love, 'tis love unbounded, \\
        Without measure, without end; \\
        Human thought is here confounded, \\
        'Tis too great to comprehend. \\
        Praise the Saviour, \\
        Magnify the sinner's Friend.

    \item While we hear the wondrous story \\
        Of the Saviour's cross and shame, \\
        Sing we, "Everlasting glory \\
        Be to God and to the Lamb." \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        Give ye glory to His name.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 71*}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. BELMONT}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item IN Christ the Lord our eyes behold \\
        A thousand glories more \\
        Than the rich gems and polished gold \\
        The sons of Aaron wore.

    \item They first their own sin-offering brought \\
        To purge themselves from sin; \\
        His life was pure, without a spot, \\
        And all His nature clean.

    \item Fresh blood as constant as the day \\
        Was on their altars spilt, \\
        But His one offering takes away \\
        For ever all our guilt.

    \item Their priesthood passed through several hands, \\
        For mortal was their race; \\
        His never-changing office stands \\
        Eternal as His days.

    \item Their range was earth, nor higher soared, \\
        The heaven of heavens is His; \\
        There, in His majesty, the Lord \\
        A Priest for ever is.

    \item Eternal glories crown His name, \\
        As Prophet, Priest, and King; \\
        Soon heaven and earth shall sound His fame, \\
        Each day fresh praises bring.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 72}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. SAMSON}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item AWAKE each soul in joyful lays \\
        And sing the great Redeemer's praise. \\
        He justly claims our melody; \\
        His loving-kindness, oh, how free!

    \item He saw us ruined in the Fall, \\
        Yet loved us notwithstanding all. \\
        He saved us from our lost estate; \\
        His loving-kindness, oh, how great!

    \item Soon shall we mount and soar away \\
        To those bright realms of endless day, \\
        And sing with rapture and surprise, \\
        His loving-kindness in the skies.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 73}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. WATTS.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Refrain: Quicker]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item COME, let us raise a joyful song \\
        Of Christ upon the throne, \\
        And join with heaven's angelic throng \\
        To make His glories known. \\
        Hallelujah for the Lamb \\
        That was slain on Mount Calvary. \\
        Hallelujah! Amen.

    \item "Worthy the One who died," they cry, \\
        "To be exalted thus;" \\
        "Worthy is He," our lips reply, \\
        "For He was slain for us."

    \item The highest honours to receive \\
        The Lord all worthy is, \\
        And blessings, more than we can give, \\
        Shall be for ever His.

    \item Let all that dwell above the sky, \\
        And air, and earth, and seas, \\
        Conspire to raise His glories high, \\
        And speak His endless praise.

    \item Creation soon shall join in one \\
        To bless the sacred name \\
        Of Him who sits upon the throne, \\
        And to exalt the Lamb.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 74}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. MEDLEY.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [CHORUS to be sung after the last verse.]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item Now in a song of grateful praise \\
        To God we will our voices raise; \\
        With gladsome hearts we'll join to tell--- \\
        Christ Jesus hath done all things well.

    \item All worlds His glorious power confess, \\
        His wisdom all His works express, \\
        But, oh, His love what tongue can tell? \\
        Christ Jesus hath done all things well.

    \item How sovereign, wonderful and free \\
        His love from all eternity! \\
        He plucked us from the jaws of hell; \\
        Christ Jesus hath done all things well.

    \item We spurned His grace, we broke His laws, \\
        And yet He undertook our cause, \\
        To save us though we did rebel; \\
        Christ Jesus hath done all things well.

    \item And when to that bright world we rise \\
        And sing our anthem in the skies, \\
        Above the rest this note shall swell--- \\
        Christ Jesus hath done all things well. \\
        And above the rest \\
        This note shall swell--- \\
        Christ Jesus hath done all things well.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 75}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.8.8.8. EATON}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item JESUS! that name all names above, \\
        Sweet passport of eternal love, \\
        Unfolding to a fallen race \\
        The wealth of God's infinite grace. \\
        What mind can grasp, what tongue proclaim \\
        The value of that peerless name?

    \item Jesus! a note of sweeter sound \\
        Not e'en in heaven itself is found; \\
        It thrills with joy the heart of God, \\
        This name of the incarnate Word. \\
        What mind can grasp, what tongue proclaim \\
        The sweetness of that peerless name?

    \item Jesus! the name that ne'er can fail \\
        To make the powers of darkness quail; \\
        To fear-wrung hearts gives lasting peace, \\
        Sin-burdened souls complete release. \\
        What mind can grasp, what tongue proclaim \\
        The prowess of that peerless name?

    \item Jesus! the name which can impart \\
        True solace to each sorrowing heart; \\
        Of death subdue the sting and gloom, \\
        With heavenly glory gild the tomb. \\
        What mind can grasp, what tongue proclaim \\
        The comfort of that peerless name?

    \item Jesus! the name through which alone \\
        Believing ones approach the throne; \\
        To God their hearts' desires outpour \\
        And draw from heaven's exhaustless store. \\
        What mind can grasp, what tongue proclaim \\
        The riches of that peerless name?

    \item Jesus! the name in which shall bow \\
        Each knee in heaven, on earth, below; \\
        God glorify with one accord, \\
        Confessing Jesus Christ as Lord. \\
        May we each day by day proclaim \\
        The glory of that peerless name.

    \item And when we pass to realms above, \\
        See Him who taught us heavenly love, \\
        With ransomed hosts enraptured raise \\
        Triumphant songs of ceaseless praise, \\
        Unhindered then shall we proclaim \\
        The virtues of that peerless name!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 76}}
    \\
    \textit{8.6.8.6.8.8.8.8. KARENS.}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD, what perfect rest is Thine! \\
        Thy rest is in Thy Son; \\
        'Tis all unspeakable, divine, \\
        Thy rest and ours are one. \\
        Inside the circle of Thy love \\
        Joined to His life are we above; \\
        How sweet with Thee, O God, to share \\
        The joy which is Thy portion there!

    \item Thou findest in that spotless One, \\
        Where all perfections dwell, \\
        All that Thy heart could wish for us, \\
        All that Thou e'er couldst tell; \\
        Thou findest ransomed, righteous, fair, \\
        Where all Thy joys transcendent are, \\
        In Him a people for Thy praise, \\
        Thy glory through eternal days.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 77}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.8.6.8.8. PISGAH.}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD of matchless grace \\
        We sing unto Thy name; \\
        We stand accepted in the place \\
        That none but Christ could claim. \\
        Our willing hearts have heard Thy voice, \\
        And in Thy mercy we rejoice.

    \item 'Tis meet that Thy delight \\
        Should centre in Thy Son; \\
        That Thou shouldst place us in Thy sight \\
        In Him Thy Holy One. \\
        Thy perfect love hath cast out fear, \\
        Thy favour shines upon us here.

    \item Eternal is our rest \\
        In Him who pleas\`ed Thee; \\
        Now of His peace, His joy, possessed, \\
        We wait His face to see. \\
        Now to Thy heart, O God, received, \\
        We know Thee whom we have believed.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 78*}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.6.8.8.6. PIETY.}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item COME, let us sing the matchless worth, \\
        And sweetly sound the praises forth, \\
        Which in the Saviour shine; \\
        To God our praise of Christ we'll bring, \\
        The song with which the heav\`ens ring \\
        We'll join through grace divine.

    \item How rich that precious blood once spilt, \\
        Our rescue from the dreadful guilt \\
        Of sin against our God! \\
        How perfect is the righteousness, \\
        In which unspotted, beauteous dress \\
        His saints have always stood!

    \item Great are the offices He bears, \\
        And bright His character appears \\
        Exalted on the throne. \\
        In songs of sweet, untiring praise \\
        We would to everlasting days \\
        Make all His glories known.

    \item And soon the happy day shall come \\
        When we shall reach our destined home, \\
        And see Him face to face; \\
        Then with our Saviour, Master, Friend, \\
        The glad eternity we'll spend, \\
        And celebrate God's grace.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 79}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. LYMINGTON.}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BY Thee, O God, invited \\
        We look unto the Son, \\
        In whom Thy soul delighted, \\
        Who all Thy will hath done; \\
        And by the one chief treasure \\
        Thy bosom freely gave, \\
        Thine own pure love we measure, \\
        Thy willing mind to save.

    \item O gracious God the Father, \\
        The one unchanging claim, \\
        The brightest hopes we gather \\
        From Christ's most precious name. \\
        What always sounds so sweetly \\
        In Thine unwearied ear, \\
        Hath freed our souls completely \\
        From all our sinful fear.

    \item The trembling sinner feareth \\
        That God can ne'er forget, \\
        But one full payment cleareth \\
        His mem'ry of all debt. \\
        When nought beside could ease us \\
        Or set our souls at large, \\
        The precious blood of Jesus \\
        Secured a full discharge.

    \item No wrath God's heart retaineth \\
        To usward who believe; \\
        No dread in ours remaineth, \\
        As we His love receive. \\
        Returning ones He kisses, \\
        And with His robe invests, \\
        His perfect love dismisses \\
        All terror from our breasts.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 80}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. MORNING LIGHT}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE sing with exultation \\
        To Thee, eternal God, \\
        Of Him who's Thy salvation, \\
        Of Jesus Christ our Lord. \\
        'Twas He who once descended \\
        More marred than men to be, \\
        Than men whom He befriended, \\
        Who pierced Him on the tree.

    \item Through Him Thy love's commended \\
        And justice satisfied; \\
        To Thee He hath ascended \\
        Whom He hath glorified; \\
        And now Thy love unbounded \\
        Is flowing full and free, \\
        And Him Thou hast surrounded \\
        With brightest majesty.

    \item Soon He will be descending \\
        For us into the air, \\
        And we in clouds ascending \\
        Caught up unto Him there; \\
        He will with exultation \\
        Set us before Thy throne \\
        Of highest exaltation \\
        Midst glories yet unknown.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 81\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{10.4.10.7.4.10. WONDERFUL LOVE}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item LORD, we adore Him who came from Thy side \\
        Long, long ago, \\
        Leaving the glory that heaven could provide, \\
        Coming to suffering and woe. \\
        Jesus our Lord--- \\
        Wonderful gift of the Father of love.

    \item We will remember Him, keeping His word--- \\
        "This must ye do;" \\
        Gladly discerning His body, O Lord, \\
        Taught by the Spirit 'tis true. \\
        Jesus our Lord--- \\
        Sacrificed, bearing our sins on the tree.

    \item We will remember His coming to earth, \\
        Immanuel! \\
        There as a Babe we behold Him at birth, \\
        God in this world here to dwell. \\
        Jesus our Lord--- \\
        Laid in a manger, eternally God!

    \item We will remember His wonderful life, \\
        Doing Thy will. \\
        Perfect obedience midst sorrow and strife \\
        Led Him to Calv\`ary's hill. \\
        Jesus our Lord--- \\
        Loving and giving each step of the way.

    \item We will remember His death on the tree, \\
        Blest be His name! \\
        Suffering the just for the unjust was He; \\
        'Twas for this purpose He came. \\
        Jesus our Lord--- \\
        Giving Himself, our Redeemer to be.

    \item We will remember He rose from the dead, \\
        Mighty to save. \\
        Living for ever, our glorious Head \\
        Conquered the power of the grave. \\
        Jesus our Lord--- \\
        Praises for Him we present unto Thee.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 82}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. D. THANKSGIVING}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HALLELUJAH! let us sing \\
        As we now thank offerings bring; \\
        "Hallelujah" is the word, \\
        Sound aloud, "Praise ye the Lord;" \\
        Hallelujah! He has given \\
        Best of gifts, His Son from heaven. \\
        Hallelujah! this is love \\
        Worthy of our God above.

    \item Hallelujah! Christ for sin \\
        Death's dark flood has entered in, \\
        Suffered all the judgment throes, \\
        Saved us from eternal woes. \\
        Hallelujah! He arose \\
        Victor over all His foes; \\
        Now exalted, glorified, \\
        Sits He at His Father's side.

    \item Hallelujah! for the grace \\
        Giving us in Christ a place, \\
        Clothing our unworthiness \\
        With God's perfect righteousness. \\
        Hallelujah! let us raise \\
        Songs resounding with His praise; \\
        Hallelujah! strike the chord, \\
        Hallelujah! Praise the Lord. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 83}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M.D. NEWCASTLE}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE countless multitude on high \\
        Who tune their songs to Jesus' name, \\
        All merit of their own deny, \\
        And Jesus' worth alone proclaim. \\
        Firm on the ground of sovereign grace \\
        They stand before Jehovah's throne; \\
        The only song in that blest place \\
        Is, "Thou art worthy, Thou alone!"

    \item Salvation's glory all be paid \\
        To Him who sits upon the throne; \\
        And to the Lamb, whose blood was shed--- \\
        "Thou, Thou art worthy, Thou alone; \\
        For Thou wast slain, and in Thy blood \\
        These robes were washed so spotless pure; \\
        Thou mad'st us kings and priests to God, \\
        For ever let Thy praise endure."

    \item Let us with joy adopt the strain \\
        We hope to sing for ever there--- \\
        "Worthy the Lamb for sinners slain, \\
        Worthy alone the crown to wear!" \\
        Without one thought that's good to plead, \\
        Oh, what could shield us from despair, \\
        But this---though we are vile indeed, \\
        The Lord our righteousness is there?

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 84}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.6.8.8.6. MANNA}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THOUGH all the beasts that live and feed \\
        Upon a thousand hills should bleed, \\
        Though all their blood should flow, \\
        The sacrifice would be in vain; \\
        The stain of sin would still remain; \\
        Sin is not cancelled so.

    \item A better sacrifice than these \\
        It needs, the conscience to appease, \\
        And satisfy the Lord. \\
        No blood hath virtue to atone \\
        For man's offence, but His alone \\
        Whose title is the Word.

    \item Jesus the Christ, on earth His name, \\
        He came, in love to sinners came, \\
        And bowed His head and died. \\
        A full atonement now is made, \\
        The ransom by His death is paid, \\
        And justice satisfied.

    \item That sinners might draw near to Him, \\
        God planned this great, this wondrous scheme, \\
        And found the ransom too. \\
        Let all the saints their voices raise, \\
        And sing the great Redeemer's praise, \\
        While endless ages flow.

\end{enumerate}
\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 4}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{The Fruits of the Accomplished Work}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 85}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M. SILCHESTER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item NOT to ourselves we owe \\
        That we, O God, are Thine; \\
        Jesus, the Sun, our night broke through \\
        And gave us light divine.

    \item The Father's grace and love \\
        This bless\`ed mercy gave; \\
        The Son came from His throne above \\
        His wandering sheep to save.

    \item No more the heirs of wrath, \\
        The smile of peace we see, \\
        And triumphing o'er sin and death \\
        We praise and worship Thee.

    \item We drink the living stream \\
        To all Thy people given, \\
        The love which Thou hast made to beam \\
        From Christ the Heir of heaven.

    \item With the adopted band, \\
        We soon shall see Him there, \\
        With Him and them before Thee stand, \\
        And in His glory share.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 86}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M.D. MONTGOMERY}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE hear the words of love, \\
        We gaze upon the blood, \\
        We see the mighty sacrifice, \\
        And we have peace with God: \\
        'Tis everlasting peace, \\
        Sure as Jehovah's name; \\
        'Tis stable as His steadfast throne, \\
        For evermore the same.

    \item The clouds may come and go, \\
        And storms may sweep our sky; \\
        This blood-sealed friendship changes not, \\
        The cross is ever nigh. \\
        Our love is ofttimes low, \\
        Our joy still ebbs and flows, \\
        But peace with Him remains the same, \\
        No change Jehovah knows.

    \item We change, He changes not; \\
        His Christ can never die; \\
        His love, not ours, the resting-place, \\
        His truth, not ours, the tie. \\
        The cross still stands unchanged, \\
        Though heaven is now His home; \\
        The mighty stone is rolled away, \\
        But yonder is His tomb.

    \item And yonder is our peace, \\
        The grave of all our woes; \\
        We know the Son of God has come, \\
        We know He died and rose. \\
        We know He liveth now \\
        At God's right hand above; \\
        We know the throne on which He sits, \\
        We know His truth and love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 87}}
    \\
    \textit{Irreg. KELSO}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item No blood, no altar now, \\
        The sacrifice is o'er; \\
        No flame, no smoke ascends on high, \\
        The lamb is slain no more; \\
        But richer blood has flowed from nobler veins, \\
        To purge the soul from guilt, and cleanse the reddest stains.

    \item We thank Thee for the blood--- \\
        The blood of Christ, Thy Son; \\
        The blood by which our peace was made, \\
        Our vict\`ory was won; \\
        Great vict\`ory o'er hell, and sin, and woe, \\
        That needs no second fight, and leaves no second foe.

    \item We thank Thee for the grace \\
        Descending from above, \\
        That overflows our widest guilt, \\
        Th' eternal Father's love, \\
        Love of the Father's co-eternal Son, \\
        Th' eternal Spirit's love---Jehovah, Three in One!

    \item We thank Thee for the hope, \\
        So glad, and sure, and clear; \\
        It holds the drooping spirit up \\
        Till the long dawn appear. \\
        Fair hope, with what a sunshine does it cheer \\
        Our roughest path on earth, our dreariest desert here!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 88}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. NEW WINCHESTER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item 'Tis we, O God, whom Thou hast shown \\
        The deadly bitterness of sin, \\
        We, who forgiving love have known, \\
        May fitly bring thank-offerings in.

    \item Thy presence called for Israel's praise, \\
        Encompassed by their mortal foes; \\
        And when in death they met their gaze \\
        What songs of glorious triumph rose!

    \item And we have known redemption, Lord, \\
        From bondage worse than theirs by far; \\
        Sin held us by a stronger cord, \\
        Yet by Thy mercy free we are.

    \item The blest Redeemer's groans and tears, \\
        His death the power of darkness broke; \\
        Bursting the chains we wore for years \\
        He freed us from the iron yoke.

    \item Divine Deliverer! He alone \\
        Thy people from the deep could bring; \\
        The glorious triumph all His own--- \\
        His name, His might, His grace we sing.

\end{enumerate}
\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 5}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{Our Access into the Holies}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 89*\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.6. EVELYN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item FOR the bread and for the wine, \\
        For the pledge that seals Him mine, \\
        For the words of love divine, \\
        We give Thee thanks, O Lord.

    \item Only bread, and only wine, \\
        Yet to faith the solemn sign \\
        Of the heavenly and divine; \\
        We give Thee thanks, O Lord.

    \item For the words that turn the eye \\
        To the cross at Calvary, \\
        Bidding us in faith draw nigh, \\
        We give Thee thanks, O Lord.

    \item For the words that fragrance breathe, \\
        These plain symbols underneath, \\
        Words that His own peace bequeath, \\
        We give Thee thanks, O Lord.

    \item For the words that tell of home, \\
        Pointing us beyond the tomb, \\
        "Do ye this until I come;" \\
        We give Thee thanks, O Lord.

    \item Till He come we take the bread, \\
        Type of Him on whom we feed, \\
        Him who liveth and was dead; \\
        We give Thee thanks, O Lord.

    \item Till He come we drink the wine, \\
        Of His blood the solemn sign, \\
        Telling each of love divine; \\
        We give Thee thanks, O Lord.

    \item For that coming here foreshown, \\
        For that day to man unknown, \\
        For the glory and the throne, \\
        We give Thee thanks, O Lord.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 90*\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.4. ALMSGIVING}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THIS broken bread and poured-out wine \\
        Sweet hallowed mem\`ories enshrine \\
        Of that great sacrifice divine--- \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

    \item Though loomed Golgotha in His view, \\
        His forethought made this statute new--- \\
        "This in remembrance of Me do;" \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

    \item The loaf He took spake of that frame, \\
        Prepared by God for Him who came \\
        To manifest His Father's name--- \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

    \item He gave God thanks, the loaf He brake; \\
        "My body this, given for your sake," \\
        Words such as these in love He spake, \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

    \item How solemn is the broken bread! \\
        Sign of His holy body, dead, \\
        Who suffered in our room and stead; \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

    \item The cup doth mem\`ories impart \\
        Of His kind sympathetic heart, \\
        For He of blood and flesh took part; \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

    \item The cup of wine poured out we see; \\
        This speaks of pardon full and free, \\
        But tells of His deep agony--- \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

    \item His woe and travail now are o'er; \\
        He who sin's dreadful burden bore \\
        Now lives triumphant evermore! \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

    \item A few times more, if so it be, \\
        We keep His words, "Remember Me," \\
        And then with joy Himself we see--- \\
        Jesus, our Lord.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 91\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M. BOYLSTON}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE bless Jehovah's name, \\
        Our sins are all forgiven; \\
        To suffer once to earth Christ came, \\
        And now He's crowned in heaven.

    \item His body offered up, \\
        His blood shed once for all, \\
        This broken loaf and poured-out cup \\
        So preciously recall.

    \item We never would forget \\
        His rich, unbounded love, \\
        Our theme of joy and wonder here, \\
        Our endless song above. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 92\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. MELCOMBE}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE never would forget His love, \\
        Who has redeemed us by His blood, \\
        And now as our High Priest above \\
        Lives as our Advocate with God.

    \item We never would forget the pain, \\
        The blood-like sweat, the shameful tree, \\
        The wrath His soul did once sustain, \\
        From sin and death to set us free.

    \item In broken bread and out-poured wine \\
        We hear Him say, "Remember Me; \\
        I gave My life, a price divine, \\
        I bore the wrath to set you free."

    \item O God, we gladly praise His love, \\
        Who suffered for us on the tree; \\
        And till He come from heaven above \\
        We keep His word---"Remember Me."

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 93\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.6.8.8.6. MARIENBERG}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item TO Thee, O God, our voice we raise, \\
        And sing Thine everlasting praise, \\
        Confessing to Thy name. \\
        How great Thou art in holiness! \\
        What majesty Thou dost possess! \\
        We celebrate Thy fame.

    \item Thou, holy everlasting God, \\
        Didst sanctify us by the blood \\
        Of Thy belov\`ed Son. \\
        Raised from the dust of earth are we \\
        To be a priesthood unto Thee, \\
        Made holy in that One.

    \item We stand before Thee in His name; \\
        O God, we have no other claim \\
        To draw nigh unto Thee. \\
        But here in Him, our holy Lord, \\
        We praise Thy name with one accord; \\
        Accepted thus are we.

    \item Our praise ascends through Christ the Lord, \\
        According to Thy holy word; \\
        The great High Priest is He. \\
        The Spirit gives us power to raise \\
        Our heart and voice in holy praise, \\
        In holy praise to Thee.

    \item We praise Thee for the matchless grace \\
        Which gives us in Thy love a place \\
        And lifts us into heaven. \\
        All glory, honour, majesty \\
        Unto Thy name for ever be \\
        For love so freely given. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 94\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. AURELIA}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE Holies now we enter \\
        In perfect peace with God, \\
        Regaining the lost centre \\
        Through Christ's atoning blood; \\
        Though great may be our dulness \\
        In thought and word and deed, \\
        We glory in the fulness \\
        Of Him who meets our need.

    \item Much incense is ascending \\
        Before th' eternal throne; \\
        God graciously is bending \\
        To listen to His own. \\
        Though feeble are our praises \\
        Christ adds His sweet perfume, \\
        And love the censer raises \\
        Their odours to consume.

    \item O God, we come with singing \\
        Because the great High Priest \\
        Our names to Thee is bringing, \\
        Nor e'er forgets the least. \\
        For us He wears the mitre \\
        Where holiness shines bright; \\
        For us His robes are whiter \\
        Than heaven's unsullied light.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 95\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. HEBER}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O BLESSED God, the Father \\
        Of Jesus Christ our Lord, \\
        In Him we are before Thee, \\
        According to Thy word; \\
        A kingdom He has made us, \\
        Priests unto Thee Most High, \\
        And we (as Thou hast bade us) \\
        As worshippers draw nigh.

    \item We meditate in wonder, \\
        O God, upon Thy love; \\
        Thy matchless grace we ponder \\
        That sent Christ from above; \\
        We think with joy, yet sadness, \\
        Of how He stooped to die, \\
        And praise Thy name with gladness \\
        He's seated now on high.

    \item We praise Thee for the savour \\
        Of His most bless\`ed name, \\
        Who brought such glory to Thee, \\
        Who did Thy love proclaim; \\
        He was for Thee an offering \\
        Devoted to Thy will; \\
        He gave Thee boundless pleasure, \\
        He Thee delighteth still.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 96\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{Irreg. WIR PFL\"{U}GEN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item INTO Thy holy presence, \\
        O God, we venture now, \\
        With reverent hearts and holy awe \\
        Before Thy throne to bow. \\
        We plead not our own virtues, \\
        They cannot here avail, \\
        But by the blood of Jesus \\
        We enter through the veil. \\
        For the gift Thou gavest, \\
        Thy Son from heaven above, \\
        We thank Thee, Lord, \\
        We praise Thee, Lord, \\
        Thou God of matchless love.

    \item No guilty fears assail us \\
        As those defiled with sin, \\
        But as a holy priesthood now \\
        With joy we enter in. \\
        Our great High Priest before us \\
        Has opened up the way, \\
        And clothed in His perfections \\
        We would our homage pay. \\
        For the gift Thou gavest, \\
        Thy Son from heaven above, \\
        We thank Thee, Lord, \\
        We praise Thee, Lord, \\
        Thou God of matchless love.

    \item Through Him our sacrifices \\
        Acceptable will be, \\
        Though not of earthly gifts received \\
        We tender unto Thee. \\
        To sacrifice of praises \\
        That unto Thee ascends, \\
        His bless\`ed name and person \\
        An incense-fragrance lends. \\
        For the gift Thou gavest, \\
        Thy Son from heaven above, \\
        We thank Thee, Lord, \\
        We praise Thee, Lord, \\
        Thou God of matchless love.

    \item We praise Thee and we worship, \\
        Thou great all-loving One, \\
        For giving up Thy Son to death \\
        For sins which we had done. \\
        His death and resurrection \\
        Have brought us nigh to Thee, \\
        Now with our hearts adoring \\
        To Thee we bow the knee. \\
        For the gift Thou gavest, \\
        Thy Son from heaven above, \\
        We thank Thee, Lord, \\
        We praise Thee, Lord, \\
        Thou God of matchless love. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 97\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. D. AUSTRIA}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GATHERED in the name Lord Jesus, \\
        Breaking bread at His command, \\
        Looking at the mystic tokens \\
        We behold and understand \\
        How for us He left the glory \\
        As a Man 'mongst men to dwell, \\
        Gave His life-blood, wondrous story, \\
        Thus to rescue us from hell.

    \item Now with holy boldness bringing \\
        Firstfruits of our lives to God, \\
        Fruit of lips we join in singing \\
        Jesus' praise with one accord. \\
        Many hearts as one united, \\
        Many voices blend in one, \\
        As we raise our loud hosannas, \\
        Praises to our God alone.

    \item Jesus' death and resurrection \\
        Is the theme that fills our heart; \\
        On the cross for our redemption \\
        He was pierced with judgment's dart. \\
        For us stricken, smitten, bruis\`ed, \\
        For us bearing sin's vast load; \\
        All the wrath of God endur\`ed, \\
        Paid our debt with His life's blood.

    \item In the silent grave they lay Him, \\
        Seal the stone and guard it well. \\
        Sheol's fetters cannot hold Him, \\
        Powerless all the hosts of hell. \\
        He comes forth o'er death victorious, \\
        See Him now the risen One, \\
        Prince of life and King of glory, \\
        Raised to heaven's eternal throne.

    \item Prophet, Priest and King, behold Him, \\
        Saviour, Shepherd, Christ and Lord; \\
        Heav\`en's myriads bow before Him, \\
        Praise His name with one accord. \\
        Saints on earth their voices raising \\
        Shout the praises, bless the name \\
        Of the One who died to save us, \\
        Jesus evermore the same.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 98\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{12.13.12.11. TRINITY}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HOLY, holy, holy, we now come before Thee, \\
        Gathered in that holy name so very dear to Thee. \\
        O Lord God, we own Thee, humbly we adore Thee, \\
        For Thy grace and power and truth and love we see.

    \item Thou Thy Son hast giv\`en, see His body riv\`en, \\
        Pierced with cru\`el thorn and spear upon Golgotha's tree. \\
        For our sins He suffered, and His body offered, \\
        And His blood outpoured a sacrifice to Thee.

    \item Now through Jesus' merit, gathered by the Spirit, \\
        Here within the holy place, through Him we boldly come; \\
        Hearts and voices blending, praise to Thee ascending, \\
        Through our great High Priest we worship Thee alone.

    \item Holy, holy, holy, humbly we adore Thee, \\
        Honour, blessing, power and might we would ascribe to Thee; \\
        Courts of heav\`en ringing with the praise we're bringing, \\
        For the One who lives our living Lord to be.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 99\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. MARYTON}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O LORD, Thy courts we humbly tread, \\
        By Thy blest Spirit hither led; \\
        We bring our sacrifice of praise, \\
        Adoring Thee in grateful lays.

    \item Chosen in Christ---oh, wondrous grace; \\
        Assured by Him a heavenly place! \\
        Ere worlds at Thy command were made, \\
        Salvation's wondrous plan was laid.

    \item How could Thy heart such love conceive--- \\
        To slay Thy Son and us receive? \\
        To charge with guilt the Holy One, \\
        Forsake Thy well-belov\`ed Son?

    \item The stream of love divine alone \\
        Through Christ could pardon and atone; \\
        Eternal ages it had flowed, \\
        And at the cross its fulness showed.

    \item Accepted through His death are we \\
        To be a priesthood, serving Thee. \\
        What joy is ours, through saving grace, \\
        To worship in the holy place!

    \item Thus boldly we now enter in, \\
        Cleansed by Christ's blood from every sin; \\
        It speaks for us before Thy throne, \\
        Proclaims redemption's work is done.

    \item Great God, Thy love all love excels; \\
        It humbles us, yet praise compels! \\
        Eternally our song shall be \\
        Of Him who said, "Remember Me."

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 100\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{6.5.6.5. D RUTH}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BASKETS full of firstfruits, \\
        Sacrifice of praise--- \\
        Songs of our Redeemer \\
        Joyfully we raise. \\
        Lord, we come before Thee \\
        Through the living way, \\
        Worshipping with gladness \\
        On this holy day.

    \item All His deeds of kindness, \\
        Every act of grace \\
        Led Him but to Calvary \\
        And the outside place. \\
        There we see Him dying, \\
        Bowing to Thy will; \\
        Lamb of Thy providing, \\
        Loving sinners still!

    \item Bought with blood most precious, \\
        Pardoned through His death, \\
        Now a holy priesthood \\
        (Thus the scripture saith), \\
        In Thy holy presence \\
        We would humbly dwell; \\
        Hearts by Christ redeem\`ed \\
        Songs of praises swell.

    \item Lord, we only offer \\
        But a thousandth part \\
        Of the love that's owed Thee \\
        From each ransomed heart. \\
        Praise we now the Father, \\
        Praise be to the Son \\
        And the Holy Spirit--- \\
        Praise the Three in One.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 101\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D PENLAN}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE come with hearts obedient, \\
        Our God, to worship Thee; \\
        The symbols we have taken \\
        In hallowed mem\`ory \\
        Of Christ, Thy Well-belov\`ed, \\
        The darling of Thy heart--- \\
        At Calv\`ary we see Him \\
        Pierced through with judgment's dart.

    \item We trace the way appointed \\
        That led to Calvary's hill, \\
        From birth to death obedient \\
        To Thy most holy will. \\
        Thyself in Him reflected, \\
        Immanuel, divine; \\
        O God, what grace and kindness \\
        In all His actions shine!

    \item Deep shadows crossed His pathway, \\
        Yet deeper still to be; \\
        We hear His cry of sorrow \\
        In dark Gethsemane. \\
        For He alone must suffer, \\
        Alone the cup must drain \\
        Of wrath and Thy forsaking, \\
        Our pardon to obtain.

    \item The Lamb of Thy providing \\
        Was on the altar laid; \\
        Our sins were laid upon Him, \\
        And thus our debt was paid. \\
        'Twas love divine that bound Him \\
        In death upon the tree, \\
        And, joined with sovereign mercy, \\
        Has set the captives free.

    \item Freed by such love, we ponder \\
        The fulness of His grace, \\
        That brings us in with gladness \\
        Within the holy place. \\
        O God, our hearts united \\
        Would praise Thee for Thy Son, \\
        For all in wondrous kindness \\
        That Thou, through Him, hast done. Amen.

\end{enumerate}
\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 6}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{God's Greatness and Goodness}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 102}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.8.8.8. MADRID}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THOU wondrous God, we love to trace \\
        The riches of Thy matchless grace; \\
        Thou, ere the worlds were made, didst plan \\
        To seek and save poor ruined man. \\
        We cannot praise Thee as we ought, \\
        Thy love excels our highest thought!

    \item Thou didst Thy Son in pity give, \\
        That sinners through His death might live; \\
        He suffered on the shameful tree \\
        To rescue us from misery. \\
        We cannot praise Him as we ought, \\
        His love excels our highest thought!

    \item The Holy Spirit, Teacher blest, \\
        Who guides us to our heavenly rest, \\
        Makes these poor hearts His dwelling place, \\
        To whisper there the tale of grace. \\
        We cannot praise Him as we ought, \\
        His love excels our highest thought!

    \item When Christ shall bring us in to Thee, \\
        We'll praise Thy grace more worthily; \\
        With all the blood-bought hosts above \\
        We'll magnify Thy boundless love; \\
        We fain would praise Thee as we ought, \\
        Thy love excels our highest thought!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 103}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M. TRENTHAM}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item STAND up and bless the Lord, \\
        Ye people of His choice; \\
        Stand up and bless the Lord your God \\
        With heart and soul and voice.

    \item Though high above all praise, \\
        Above all blessing high, \\
        Who would not fear His holy name, \\
        And laud and magnify?

    \item Oh, for the living flame \\
        From His own altar brought, \\
        To touch our lips, our mind inspire, \\
        And wing to heaven our thought!

    \item God is our strength and song, \\
        And His salvation ours; \\
        Then be His love in Christ proclaimed \\
        With all our ransomed powers.

    \item Stand up and bless the Lord, \\
        The Lord your God adore; \\
        Stand up and bless His glorious name \\
        Henceforth for evermore.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 104}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. EVAN}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD, how wide Thy glory shines, \\
        How high Thy wonders rise, \\
        Known through the earth by thousand signs, \\
        By thousands through the skies!

    \item Those mighty orbs proclaim Thy power, \\
        Their motions speak Thy skill; \\
        And on the wings of every hour \\
        We read Thy patience still.

    \item But when we view Thy great design \\
        To save rebellious worms, \\
        Our souls are filled with awe divine \\
        To see what God performs.

    \item For the bright glories of Christ's name \\
        Fill heaven and earth with praise; \\
        And angels learn Immanuel's fame, \\
        And celebrate His grace.

    \item Oh let us bear our humble part \\
        In that eternal song, \\
        Let joy and wonder tune each heart \\
        And love command each tongue.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 105}}
    \\
    \textit{11.11.11.11. ST. DENIO}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item IMMORTAL, invisible, God only wise, \\
        In light inaccessible hid from our eyes, \\
        Most bless\`ed, most glorious, the Ancient of days, \\
        Almighty, victorious, Thy great name we praise.

    \item Unresting, unhasting, and silent as light, \\
        Nor wanting, nor wasting, Thou rulest in might; \\
        Thy justice like mountains high soaring above, \\
        Thy clouds are as fountains of goodness and love.

    \item To all life Thou givest, to great and to small; \\
        In all life Thou livest, the true life of all. \\
        We blossom and flourish as leaves on the tree, \\
        And wither and perish, but nought changeth Thee.

    \item Great Father of glory, pure Father of light, \\
        Thine angels adore Thee, all veiling their sight; \\
        All praise we would render, Oh help us to see \\
        'Tis only the splendour of light hideth Thee.

    \item Immortal, invisible, God only wise, \\
        In light inaccessible hid from our eyes, \\
        Most bless\`ed, most glorious, the Ancient of days, \\
        Almighty, victorious, Thy great name we praise.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 106}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. WAREHAM}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item JEHOVAH reigns, He dwells in light, \\
        Girded with majesty and might. \\
        The world, created by His hands, \\
        Firm on its first foundation stands.

    \item But ere this spacious world was made \\
        Or had its first foundation laid, \\
        Thy throne eternal ages stood, \\
        Thyself the ever-living God.

    \item For ever shall Thy throne endure, \\
        Thy promise stands for ever sure; \\
        And holiness to endless days \\
        Becomes the dwelling of Thy grace.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 107}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. HOLLEY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item STAND and adore! how glorious He \\
        That dwells in bright eternity; \\
        We gaze and we confound our sight, \\
        Plunged in th' abyss of dazzling light.

    \item Thou Sacred One, Almighty Three, \\
        Great everlasting mystery, \\
        What lofty numbers shall we frame, \\
        Equal to Thy tremendous name?

    \item Seraphs that stand around the throne \\
        Begin and speak the great Unknown; \\
        Attempt the song, wind up your strings \\
        To notes untried and boundless things.

    \item Ye whose capacious powers survey \\
        Largely beyond our eyes of clay, \\
        Yet what a narrow portion too \\
        Is seen, or known, or thought by you!

    \item How flat your highest praises fall \\
        Below th' immense Original! \\
        Weak creatures we that strive in vain \\
        To reach an uncreated strain!

    \item Great God, forgive our feeble lays, \\
        Sound out Thine own eternal praise; \\
        A song so vast, a theme so high, \\
        Calls for the voice that tuned the sky. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 108}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. WIMBORNE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD, we look to Thee above, \\
        Before Thy holy throne we bow; \\
        We worship Thee, Thou God of love, \\
        Adoring homage bring Thee now.

    \item Thou art the everlasting God, \\
        Past, present, future, still the same. \\
        Creation by Thy mighty word \\
        Displays the wonders of Thy name.

    \item Thou triune God, there's none beside; \\
        With Thee the honour none can share. \\
        Far, far above earth's pomp and pride, \\
        With Thee none ever can compare.

    \item Yet One from Thee, oh, matchless grace, \\
        Oh, wondrous mystery divine, \\
        In human form did take man's place, \\
        And God and man in one combine!

    \item The Christ of God, in Him we see \\
        God's bright effulgence shining forth; \\
        And nailed upon Golgotha's tree \\
        A sacrifice of boundless worth.

    \item And now He lives on high enthroned, \\
        Yes, now He lives no more to die. \\
        He who by death for sin atoned \\
        Now lives to bring His people nigh.

    \item Through Him to God we now draw near, \\
        Of Him to God our praises bring. \\
        God for His sake will deign to hear, \\
        And to accept the songs we sing.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 109}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. HURSLEY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SWEET is the work, our God and King, \\
        To praise Thy name, give thanks and sing, \\
        To show Thy love by morning light, \\
        And tell of all Thy truth by night.

    \item Our hearts shall triumph in Thee, Lord, \\
        And bless Thy works and bless Thy word; \\
        Thy works of grace, how bright they shine, \\
        How deep Thy counsels, how divine!

    \item But if the little that we know \\
        Of Thee and Thine while here below \\
        Such triumph gives, what will it be \\
        When face to face Thyself we see?

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 110}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. FESTUS}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THOU God of glorious majesty, \\
        What can we render unto Thee \\
        For all that Thou for us hast done \\
        In Christ Thy well-belov\`ed Son?

    \item When Israel's sons Thy presence sought, \\
        Acceptably to Thee they brought, \\
        As meet thank-offerings to be, \\
        The best of that received from Thee.

    \item As reverently Thy courts they trod \\
        They praised and worshipped Thee, their God, \\
        For vict\`ories Thine arm achieved, \\
        For earthly benefits received.

    \item Then what more fitting now can we \\
        Give, O Thou bless\`ed God, to Thee \\
        Than of the best that Thou hast given, \\
        Thy gift of gifts sent down from heaven?

    \item We make confession to His name, \\
        Who, loving and obedient, came \\
        To do Thy will, to meet our need, \\
        For Thee to live, for us to bleed.

    \item Though fails our understanding dull \\
        His worth to compass to the full, \\
        We yet would speak of Him to Thee; \\
        Dear to Thyself and us is He.

    \item We would adore Thee, God of love, \\
        Because of Him enthroned above, \\
        Through whom Thou hast Thyself made known, \\
        Thy purposes eternal shown.

    \item In His blest name our voice we raise \\
        In songs of gratitude and praise; \\
        Be Thou for evermore adored, \\
        Thou holy, wise, all-loving Lord.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 111}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. MASON}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SEE mercy, mercy from on high \\
        Descend to rebels doomed to die. \\
        'Tis mercy free which knows no bound, \\
        How grand, how gladsome is the sound!

    \item Soon as the reign of sin began \\
        The light of mercy dawned on man, \\
        When God announced the early news, \\
        ``The woman's Seed thy head shall bruise.''

    \item Brightly it beamed on man forlorn, \\
        When Christ, the holy Child, was born, \\
        And in its fullest splendour shone \\
        When Jesus, dying, cried ``'Tis done.''

    \item It triumphed when from death He rose \\
        And brake the power of all His foes; \\
        And since He took His seat on high \\
        Now mercy reigns eternally.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 112}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.8.8.8. SOVEREIGNTY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GREAT God of wonders, all Thy ways \\
        Are worthy of Thyself, divine, \\
        But the bright glories of Thy grace \\
        Beyond Thine other wonders shine. \\
        Who is a pardoning God like Thee, \\
        Or who hath grace so rich and free?

    \item Such deep transgressions to forgive, \\
        Such guilty daring worms to spare--- \\
        This is Thy grand prerogative, \\
        And in this honour none shall share. \\
        Who is a pardoning God like Thee, \\
        Or who hath grace so rich and free?

    \item Pardon from an offended God, \\
        Pardon for sins of deepest dye, \\
        Pardon bestowed through Jesus' blood, \\
        Pardon that brings the rebel nigh; \\
        Who is a pardoning God like Thee, \\
        Or who hath grace so rich and free?

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 113}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. ADORATION}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GIVE thanks to God Most High, \\
        The universal Lord, \\
        The Sovereign King of kings, \\
        And be His name adored. \\
        Thy mercy, Lord, shall aye endure; \\
        Thy word abides for ever sure.

    \item How mighty is His hand! \\
        What wonders He hath done! \\
        He formed the earth and seas \\
        And spread the heaven alone. \\
        Thy power and grace are still the same; \\
        Let endless praise exalt Thy name.

    \item His wisdom framed the sun \\
        To crown the day with light, \\
        The moon and twinkling stars \\
        To cheer the darksome night. \\
        Thy mercy, Lord, shall aye endure; \\
        Thy word abides for ever sure.

    \item He smote the first-born sons, \\
        The flower of Egypt, dead; \\
        And thence the chosen tribes \\
        With joy and glory led. \\
        Thy power and grace are still the same; \\
        Let endless praise exalt Thy name.

    \item He sent His only Son \\
        To save us from our woe, \\
        From Satan, sin, and death \\
        And every hurtful foe. \\
        Thy mercy, Lord, shall aye endure; \\
        Thy word abides for ever sure.

    \item Give thanks aloud to God, \\
        To God the heavenly King; \\
        With all around His throne \\
        His works and glories sing. \\
        Thy power and grace are still the same; \\
        Let endless praise exalt Thy name.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 114}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.8.4. D. ABRAHAM}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SALVATION to our God \\
        Who did provide the Lamb--- \\
        The merit of His precious blood \\
        Our only claim. \\
        Our God salvation gives, \\
        And through His Son it flows; \\
        Once slain for us, for us He lives, \\
        Our sole repose.

    \item He who was slain is seen \\
        On God's eternal throne; \\
        And His redeemed are white and clean \\
        Through Him alone. \\
        Salvation's joyful sound \\
        Bursts from the blood-bought throng, \\
        And soon creation all around \\
        Shall join the song.

    \item Our hearts are tuned for this, \\
        Glad songs our tongues employ; \\
        Christ is the spring of all our bliss, \\
        And God our joy. \\
        Salvation to our God, \\
        Thanksgiving, power and might, \\
        For Him who cleansed us by His blood, \\
        Our Life and Light. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 115}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.6.8.8.6. PEMBROKE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THOU God of power and God of love, \\
        The seraphs in the realms above \\
        To Thee their praises bring, \\
        And veil their faces while they cry, \\
        ``Thrice holy'' to their God Most High, \\
        ``Thrice holy'' to their King.

    \item Thee as our God we too would claim, \\
        And bless the precious Saviour's name \\
        Through whom this grace is given; \\
        Who bore the wrath to sinners due, \\
        Who formed our ruined souls anew, \\
        And made us heirs of heaven.

    \item Give us to know the joy and love \\
        With which all worship Thee above \\
        In heaven, Thy blest abode. \\
        Here to our hearts Thyself reveal, \\
        And all assembled cause to feel \\
        The presence of our God.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 116}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. VIENNA}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GREAT Jehovah, Living One, \\
        On Thy high eternal throne, \\
        Thine almighty majesty \\
        Calls for homage due to Thee.

    \item Dwelling in the light intense, \\
        Holy in omnipotence, \\
        All Thy ways are righteousness, \\
        We with reverence would confess.

    \item Thine unequalled wisdom shines \\
        Gloriously in Thy designs; \\
        All things working to fulfil \\
        Counsels of Thy sovereign will.

    \item God of providence! Thy care, \\
        Shown by gifts Thy creatures share, \\
        Bids Thy vast creation raise \\
        One harmonious song of praise.

    \item Father of our bless\`ed Lord, \\
        Ever be Thy name adored \\
        For that One of matchless worth, \\
        Who so fully told Thee forth.

    \item In the work He wrought we see \\
        Power and wisdom, wondrously \\
        Joined with love and righteousness, \\
        Us eternally to bless.

    \item For the life He lived below, \\
        For the cross with all its woe, \\
        For His glorious vict\`ory, \\
        We would praise and worship Thee.

    \item[\textbf{8.\textdagger{}}] Great Jehovah throned on high, \\
        By Thy bless\`ed Son brought nigh, \\
        Cleansed and clothed as priests we bring \\
        In His name thank-offering.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 117}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.7. REGENT SQUARE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SOVEREIGN grace o'er sin abounding, \\
        Ransomed souls the tidings swell; \\
        'Tis a deep that knows no sounding, \\
        Who its breadth or length can tell? \\
        On its glories \\
        Let our souls for ever dwell.

    \item What from Christ the soul can sever, \\
        Bound by everlasting bands? \\
        Once in Him, in Him for ever--- \\
        Thus th' eternal covenant stands. \\
        None can pluck us \\
        From the Strength of Israel's hands.

    \item Love eternal in Christ chose us, \\
        Long ere time its race began. \\
        Wealth of heavenly blessing shows us \\
        What the love of God has done; \\
        With Christ Jesus, \\
        By eternal union one.

    \item On such love let us still ponder, \\
        Love so great, so rich, so free; \\
        Say, while lost in holy wonder, \\
        ``Why are we, Lord, loved by Thee?'' \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        Grace shall reign eternally.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 118}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.7. BENEDICTION}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item 'TWAS Thy love, O God, that knew us \\
        Earth's foundation long before; \\
        That same love to Jesus drew us \\
        By its sweet constraining power, \\
        And will keep us \\
        Safely now and evermore.

    \item God of love, our souls adore Thee, \\
        We would still Thy grace proclaim, \\
        Till we cast our crowns before Thee \\
        And in glory praise Thy name. \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        Be to God and to the Lamb.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 119}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.7.7. ROUSSEAU}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SOFT the voice of mercy sounded \\
        Sweet as music to the ear; \\
        ``Grace abounds where sin abounded,'' \\
        This the word that soothed our fear. \\
        Grace, the sweetest sound we know, \\
        Grace to sinners here below.

    \item Grace we sing, God's grace through Jesus, \\
        Grace, the spring of peace to man; \\
        Grace that from each sorrow frees us, \\
        Grace too high for thought to scan; \\
        Grace, the theme of God's own love, \\
        Grace, the theme all themes above.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 120}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.5. CAPETOWN}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item PRAISE we now the Father's love--- \\
        From His lofty throne above \\
        He has sent the heavenly Dove, \\
        Paraclete divine.

    \item Seated at the Father's side, \\
        Raised, exalted, glorified, \\
        Christ has sent Him to abide, \\
        Paraclete divine.

    \item Sent by God in Jesus' name, \\
        Willingly the Spirit came; \\
        We His majesty acclaim, \\
        Paraclete divine.

    \item Three in One and One in Three, \\
        God Almighty, unto Thee \\
        Worship, power, dominion be \\
        Now and aye; Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 121}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. EVENING HYMN}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BLEST Trinity, from mortal sight \\
        Veiled in Thine own eternal light, \\
        We Thee confess, in Thee believe, \\
        To Thee with loving hearts we cleave.

    \item O God the Father, Holy One, \\
        With Thee is Thine eternal Son, \\
        And Holy Spirit, evermore \\
        The Three in One whom we adore.

    \item The Father is in God the Son, \\
        And with the Father He is one; \\
        In Both the Spirit doth abide, \\
        And with Them Both is glorified.

    \item Such as the Father, such the Son, \\
        And such the Spirit, Three in One. \\
        The Three one perfect Verity, \\
        The Three one perfect Charity.

    \item Three Persons praise we evermore, \\
        One only God our hearts adore. \\
        Thou Trinity, Thou Unity, \\
        Now let our praise accepted be. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 122\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{11.12.12.10. NIC{\AE}A}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HOLY, holy, holy, Lord God Almighty, \\
        On this Lordly morning our song we raise to Thee; \\
        Holy, holy, holy, merciful and mighty, \\
        God in three Persons, bless\`ed Trinity.

    \item Holy, holy, holy, all the saints adore Thee, \\
        Worshipping with gladness we now make melody; \\
        Cherubim and seraphim falling down before Thee, \\
        Who wert, and art, and evermore shalt be.

    \item Holy, holy, holy, though Thy brightness hide Thee, \\
        Though the eye of mortal man Thy glory cannot see, \\
        Only Thou art holy, there is none beside Thee, \\
        Perfect in power, in love and purity.

    \item Holy, holy, holy, Lord God Almighty, \\
        All Thy works shall praise Thy name, in earth and sky and sea: \\
        Holy, holy, holy, merciful and mighty, \\
        God in three Persons, bless\`ed Trinity. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 123\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{11.10.11.10. D. CAMPBELL}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately Fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item PRAISE ye Jehovah, praise the Lord most holy, \\
        Who cheers the contrite, girds with strength the weak; \\
        Praise Him who will with glory crown the lowly, \\
        And with salvation beautify the meek. \\
        Praise ye the Lord for all His loving-kindness, \\
        And all the tender mercies He has shown; \\
        Praise Him who pardons all our sins and blindness, \\
        And calls us sons and takes us for His own. \\
        Holy, holy, holy, Lord God Almighty, \\
        On this Lordly morn our song we raise to Thee; \\
        Holy, holy, holy, merciful and mighty, \\
        God in three Persons, bless\`ed Trinity.

    \item Praise ye Jehovah, source of every blessing, \\
        Viewed with His gifts, earth's brightest boons are dim; \\
        Resting on Him, His peace and joy possessing, \\
        All things are ours, for we have all in Him. \\
        Praise ye the Father, God the Lord, who gave us \\
        With full and perfect love His only Son; \\
        Praise ye the Son, who died Himself to save us; \\
        Praise ye the Spirit; praise the Three in One. \\
        Holy, holy, holy, Lord God Almighty, \\
        On this Lordly morn our song we raise to Thee; \\
        Holy, holy, holy, merciful and mighty, \\
        God in three Persons, bless\`ed Trinity. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 7}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{Doxologies and Chants}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 124}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M.D. BETHLEHEM}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item UNTO the One who hath been slain \\
        Be endless blessings paid, \\
        Salvation, glory, joy remain \\
        For ever on His head. \\
        He hath redeemed us with His blood, \\
        Hath set the prisoners free, \\
        And we will praise His name, O God, \\
        To all eternity. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 125}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. ST. SWITHIN}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item ON earth the song begins, \\
        In heaven more sweet and loud; \\
        To Him who cleansed our sins \\
        By His atoning blood, \\
        To Him (we sing in joyful strain) \\
        Be honour, power and praise, Amen.

    \item Alone He bare the cross, \\
        Alone its grief sustained; \\
        His was the shame and loss, \\
        And He the victory gained. \\
        The mighty work was all His own, \\
        Though we shall share His glorious throne.

    \item To Him who bare the cross, \\
        Who suffered shame and loss, \\
        And the great victory gained, \\
        To Him (we sing in joyful strain) \\
        Be honour, power and praise, Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 126}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.4.7. RUSSIA}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Slow]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item UNTO Him who loved us, gave us \\
        Every pledge that love could give, \\
        Freely shed His blood to save us, \\
        Gave His life that we might live, \\
        Be the kingdom \\
        And dominion, \\
        And the glory evermore. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 127}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.4.4. GLORY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GLORY, honour, praise and pow\`er \\
        Be unto the Lamb for ever. \\
        Jesus Christ is our Redeemer, \\
        Hallelujah! \\
        Praise ye the Lord! Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 128}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.6.6.6.5. HANOVER}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD, we adore Him \\
        Who once was the slain One, \\
        Now living for ever \\
        Enthron\`ed in heav\`en. \\
        O God, we adore Him, \\
        For He has redeemed us; \\
        Our title to glory \\
        We read in His blood.

    \item O God, we acknowledge \\
        The depth of Thy riches, \\
        For of Thee and through Thee \\
        And to Thee are all things. \\
        How rich is Thy mercy, \\
        How great Thy salvation! \\
        We bless Thee, we praise Thee; \\
        Amen, amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 129}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.6.6.6.5. LION OF JUDAH}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item To Him who is able \\
        To keep us---His called ones, \\
        Preserved in Christ Jesus, \\
        The saints of the Father--- \\
        To keep us from falling, \\
        And faultless to set us \\
        Before His bright glory \\
        With fulness of joy,

    \item To the Lord God, who keepeth \\
        Midst sin and in weakness, \\
        Whose wisdom alone is, \\
        To God and our Saviour \\
        Be majesty, glory, \\
        Dominion and pow\`er, \\
        Both now and for ever, \\
        Amen, amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage

        Bless\`ed be Jehovah's name \\
        From this time forth and for evermore.

    \item From the rising to the setting of the sun, \\
        Jehovah's name is to be prais\`ed. \\
        Jehovah is high above all nations, \\
        And His glory above the heavens.

    \item Who is like unto Jehovah our God, \\
        That hath His seat on high? \\
        He raiseth up the poor man out of the dust, \\
        And lifteth up the needy from the dunghill;

    \item That He may make him sit with princes, \\
        Even with the princes of His people. \\
        Praise ye Jah, bless\`ed be Jehovah's name \\
        From this time forth and for evermore.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 146\textdagger{}}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.10.10. EVENTIDE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HERE, bless\`ed God, we see Him face to face; \\
        Here faith can touch and handle things unseen; \\
        Here would we grasp with firmer hands His grace, \\
        And all our weariness upon Him lean.

    \item Here would we feed upon the bread of God, \\
        Here drink with Him the royal wine of heaven; \\
        Here would we lay aside each earthly load, \\
        Here taste afresh the calm of sin forgiven.

    \item We have no help but His, nor do we need \\
        Another arm save His to lean upon, \\
        It is enough, O God, enough indeed, \\
        Our strength is in His might, His might alone.

    \item Ours is the sin, but His the righteousness, \\
        Ours is the guilt, but His the cleansing blood. \\
        Here is our robe, our refuge, and our peace, \\
        The righteousness He wrought, O Lord our God.

    \item Too soon we rise, the symbols disappear, \\
        The feast, though not the love, is past and gone; \\
        The bread and wine remove, but He is here, \\
        Nearer than ever, still our Shield and Sun.

    \item Feast after feast thus comes and passes by, \\
        Yet passing points to the glad feast above, \\
        Giving sweet foretastes of the festal joy, \\
        The Lamb's great bridal feast of bliss and love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 147}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. ST. AIDAN (German)}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Slow]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item AMEN, one lasting, long Amen--- \\
        Blest anthem of eternal days; \\
        The fulness of the rapturous song \\
        To Christ the Saviour's endless praise.

    \item Amen, one lasting, long Amen--- \\
        Heaven's blissful cadence, deep and loud, \\
        While every heart before the throne \\
        In holy, solemn awe is bowed.

    \item Amen, Amen, it rolls along, \\
        Re-echoing from the throne again; \\
        Be ours to mingle with the throng \\
        In that eternal, loud Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 148}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.4.4.4.4. RICHMOND}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item LORD of the worlds above, \\
        How pleasant and how fair \\
        The dwellings of Thy love, \\
        The heavenly mansions are! \\
        To Thine abode \\
        Our hearts aspire \\
        With warm desire \\
        To see our God.

    \item There is Thy throne of grace, \\
        And there the sprinkled blood; \\
        There lives before Thy face \\
        Our great High Priest, O God; \\
        His name our plea, \\
        We now draw near \\
        In holy fear \\
        To worship Thee.

    \item We go from strength to strength \\
        Through this dark vale of tears, \\
        Till each arrives at length \\
        And safe in heaven appears. \\
        Oh, glorious seat, \\
        Where God the King \\
        Shall shortly bring \\
        Our willing feet!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 149}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. WEBER}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item CHRIST has done the mighty work; \\
        Nothing left for us to do \\
        But to enter on His toil, \\
        Enter on His triumph too.

    \item His the pardon, ours the sin--- \\
        Great the sin, the pardon great; \\
        His the good, and ours the ill; \\
        His the love, and ours the hate.

    \item His the labour, ours the r\`est; \\
        His the death, and ours the life; \\
        Ours the fruits of vict\`ory, \\
        His the agony and strife.

    \item He has sown the precious seed, \\
        Nothing left for us unsown; \\
        Ours it is to reap the field, \\
        Make the harvest joy our own.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 150}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.10.10. PAX DEI}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item JEHOVAH God the Father, bless and keep \\
        His little flock of feeble lambs and sheep; \\
        Jehovah God the Son, His glorious face \\
        Make shine on us, so full of kindly grace;

    \item Jehovah God the Paraclete, uplift \\
        His countenance, and grant us peace, His gift. \\
        Eternal triune God, with love inflame \\
        Our souls, to praise Thy great Jehovah name. Amen, amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 151}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6. QUAM DILECTA}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item ONCE more before we part, \\
        We bless the Saviour's name; \\
        Let every tongue and heart \\
        Join to extol the Lamb.

    \item We on Thy holy word \\
        Would feed, and live, and grow; \\
        Go on to know Thee, Lord, \\
        And practise what we know.

    \item Lord, in Thy grace we came; \\
        Thy blessing still impart. \\
        We met in Thine own name \\
        And in Thy name we part.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 152}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. JACKSON'S}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THIS is the day the Lord hath made, \\
        He calls the hours His own. \\
        Let heaven rejoice, let earth be glad, \\
        And praise surround Thy throne.

    \item To-day He rose and left the dead, \\
        And Satan's empire fell; \\
        To-day the saints His triumph spread, \\
        And all His wonders tell.

    \item Help us, O Lord, on this blest day, \\
        To spread the glad, good news, \\
        And may Thy gospel's healing ray \\
        Its saving power diffuse.

    \item Hosanna to th' anointed King, \\
        To God's most holy Son! \\
        With gladsome hearts we now would bring \\
        Our praise unto the throne.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 9}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{God and His Grace}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 153}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M. MARCHING TO ZION}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item COME, ye that love the Lord, \\
        And let your joys be known; \\
        Join in a song with sweet accord, \\
        And thus surround the throne.

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{We're marching to Zion, beautiful, beautiful Zion!} \\
    \textit{We're marching upward to Zion,} \\
