    \textit{The beautiful city of God.}
    \vspace{0.4em}

    \item Let those refuse to sing \\
        Who never knew our God; \\
        But children of the heavenly King \\
        Must speak their joys abroad.

    \item The hill of Zion yields \\
        A thousand sacred sweets, \\
        Before we reach the heavenly fields \\
        Or walk the golden streets.

    \item Then let our songs abound, \\
        And every tear be dry, \\
        We're marching through this weary land \\
        To fairer worlds on high.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 154}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M. FALCON STREET}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GRACE! 'tis a charming sound, \\
        Harmonious to the ear; \\
        Heaven with the echo shall resound, \\
        And all the earth shall hear.

    \item Grace first contrived the way \\
        To save rebellious man, \\
        And all the steps that grace display \\
        Which drew the wondrous plan.

    \item 'Twas grace that wrote each name \\
        In life's eternal book; \\
        'Twas grace that gave us to the Lamb, \\
        Who all our sorrows took.

    \item Grace taught our wandering feet \\
        To tread the heavenly road; \\
        And new supplies each hour we meet \\
        While travelling home to God.

    \item Grace taught us how to pray, \\
        And made our eyes o'erflow; \\
        'Tis grace has kept us to this day, \\
        And will not let us go.

    \item May grace, free grace, inspire \\
        Our souls with strength divine; \\
        May all our thoughts to God aspire, \\
        And grace in service shine.

    \item Grace all the work shall crown \\
        Through everlasting days; \\
        It lays in heaven the topmost stone, \\
        And well deserves the praise.

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{Chorus at the end of the hymn.} \\
    \textit{Praise ye the Lord, Hallelujah!} \\
    \textit{Hallelujah!} \\
    \textit{Hallelujah! Praise ye the Lord. Amen.}
    \vspace{0.4em}

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 155}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. COLCHESTER}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item COME, ye that know the Saviour's name, \\
        And raise your thoughts above; \\
        Let every heart and voice unite \\
        To sing that God is love.

    \item His word this precious truth reveals, \\
        And all His mercies prove; \\
        Creation and redemption join \\
        To show that God is love.

    \item His patience bearing much and long \\
        With those who from Him rove, \\
        His kindness when He leads them home, \\
        Attest that God is love.

    \item The work begun is carried on \\
        By power from heaven above, \\
        And every step from first to last \\
        Declares that God is love.

    \item Oh, may we all while here below \\
        His perfect will approve, \\
        Till nobler songs in brighter worlds \\
        Proclaim that God is love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 156}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. HOWARD}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WHEN all Thy mercies, bless\`ed God, \\
        Each rising soul surveys, \\
        Transported with the view, we're lost \\
        In wonder, love and praise.

    \item Unnumbered comforts on each soul \\
        Thy tender care bestowed, \\
        Before our infant hearts conceived \\
        From whom those comforts flowed.

    \item Ten thousand thousand precious gifts \\
        Our daily thanks employ, \\
        But oh! Thy greatest gift, Thy Son, \\
        This is our greatest joy.

    \item Through all eternity to Thee \\
        A joyful song we'll raise, \\
        But e'en eternity's too short \\
        To utter all Thy praise.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 157}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M.D. PETERBOROUGH}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE spacious firmament on high, \\
        With all the blue ethereal sky, \\
        And spangled heavens, a shining frame, \\
        Their great Original proclaim. \\
        Th' unwearied sun from day to day \\
        Does his Creator's power display, \\
        And publishes to every land \\
        The work of an almighty hand.

    \item Soon as the evening shades prevail \\
        The moon takes up the wondrous tale, \\
        And nightly to the listening earth \\
        Repeats the story of her birth; \\
        While all the stars that round her burn, \\
        And all the planets in their turn, \\
        Confirm the tidings as they roll, \\
        And spread the truth from pole to pole.

    \item What though in solemn silence all \\
        Move round the dark terrestrial ball? \\
        What though no real voice or sound \\
        Amidst their radiant orbs be found? \\
        In reason's ear they all rejoice \\
        And utter forth a glorious voice; \\
        For ever singing as they shine, \\
        "The hand that made us is divine."

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 158}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7.7.7. MONKLAND}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item LET us with a gladsome mind \\
        Praise the Lord, for He is kind; \\
        For His mercies aye endure, \\
        Ever faithful, ever sure.

    \item He, with all commanding might, \\
        Filled the formless earth with light; \\
        For His mercies aye endure, \\
        Ever faithful, ever sure.

    \item All things living He doth feed, \\
        His full hand supplies their need; \\
        For His mercies aye endure, \\
        Ever faithful, ever sure.

    \item He hath with a piteous eye \\
        Looked upon our mis\`ery; \\
        For His mercies aye endure, \\
        Ever faithful, ever sure.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 159}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. OMBERSLEY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item OUR God, we call upon our souls \\
        And all within us that subsists, \\
        To bless and praise Thy holy name, \\
        Nor to forget Thy benefits.

    \item All our iniquities and sins, \\
        In mercy great Thou hast forgiven, \\
        And healest, too, diseases all \\
        Of our frail frame by boons from heaven.

    \item Our lives are now redeemed by Thee \\
        From all destruction, and we're crowned \\
        With loving-kindness full and free, \\
        And tender mercies which abound.

    \item Thou also satisfiest well \\
        Our hungry mouths with all good thing \\
        So that our youth becomes renewed, \\
        Like eagles as they mount on wings.

    \item And though we're likened unto grass, \\
        Whose flower by wind doth pass away, \\
        Thy loving-kindness endeth not \\
        For fearing ones who heed Thy way.

    \item O gracious God, Thy throne's on high, \\
        Thy kingdom ruleth over all. \\
        We join the great angelic hosts \\
        To bless Thee and before Thee fall.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 160}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.8.8.8. ST. CHRYSOSTOM}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD, whose wondrous name is Love, \\
        Whose hands have fashioned us anew, \\
        Before Thy face is now the Lamb, \\
        Whom sinful man once pierced and slew; \\
        Thine holy Son Thou didst not spare, \\
        How canst Thou cease for us to care?

    \item Our heavenly Father, grant us all \\
        The new-born babe's simplicity. \\
        The doubtful mind be far from us \\
        Who boast a God that cannot lie. \\
        Arrayed in comeliness divine, \\
        On Jesus' bosom we recline.

    \item Thou art the Potter, we the clay, \\
        Thy will be ours, Thy truth our light; \\
        Thy love the fountain of our joy, \\
        Thine arm our safeguard day and night, \\
        Till Thou shalt wipe our tears away, \\
        And Christ shall bring eternal day.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 161}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.8.4. D. LEONI}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE God of Abraham praise, \\
        Who reigns enthroned above, \\
        Ancient of everlasting days, \\
        And God of love. \\
        Jehovah, great I AM, \\
        By earth and heaven confessed; \\
        We bow, and bless the sacred name, \\
        For ever blest.

    \item The God of Abraham praise, \\
        At whose supreme command \\
        From earth we rise, and seek the joys \\
        At His right hand. \\
        He calls us to forsake \\
        Earth's wisdom, fame, and power; \\
        And Him our only portion make, \\
        Our shield and tower.

    \item The God of Abraham praise, \\
        Whose all-sufficient grace \\
        Shall guide us all our earthly days, \\
        In all our ways. \\
        He calls a worm His friend; \\
        He calls Himself our God; \\
        And He shall save us to the end, \\
        Through Jesus' blood.

    \item He by Himself hath sworn, \\
        We on His oath depend; \\
        We shall on eagles' wings upborne, \\
        To heaven ascend. \\
        We shall behold His face, \\
        We shall His power adore, \\
        And sing the wonders of His grace, \\
        For evermore.

    \item The whole triumphant host \\
        Give thanks to God on high; \\
        "Thrice holy Thou, Jehovah God," \\
        They ever cry. \\
        We join the heavenly song; \\
        Our God and Abraham's praise, \\
        "Might, majesty to Thee belong, \\
        To endless days."

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 162}}
    \\
    \textit{8.3.8.3.8.8.8.3. LYTH}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item COME let us all unite to sing, \\
        God is love. \\
        Let heaven and earth their praises bring; \\
        God is love. \\
        Let every soul from sin awake, \\
        Each in his heart sweet music make, \\
        And sing with us for Jesus' sake, \\
        God is love.

    \item Oh, tell to earth's remotest bound, \\
        God is love. \\
        In Christ we have redemption found; \\
        God is love. \\
        His blood has put our sins away, \\
        His Spirit turned our night to day, \\
        And now we can rejoice to say, \\
        God is love.

    \item How happy is our portion here! \\
        God is love. \\
        His promises our spirits cheer; \\
        God is love. \\
        He is our sun and shield by day, \\
        Our help, our hope, our strength and stay, \\
        He will be with us all the way; \\
        God is love.

    \item In glory we shall sing again, \\
        God is love. \\
        Yes, this shall be our lofty strain, \\
        God is love. \\
        Whilst endless ages roll along, \\
        In concert with the heavenly throng \\
        This shall be still our sweetest song, \\
        God is love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 163}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.4. HANFORD}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WE cannot always trace the way \\
        Where Thou, our gracious Lord, dost move; \\
        But we can always surely say \\
        That Thou art love.

    \item When fear its gloomy cloud will fling \\
        O'er earth; our souls to heaven above, \\
        As to their sanctuary, spring; \\
        For Thou art love.

    \item When mystery shrouds our darkened path, \\
        We'll check our dread, our doubts remove; \\
        In this each saint sweet comfort hath--- \\
        That Thou art love.

    \item Yes, Thou art love. A truth like this \\
        Can every gloomy thought remove, \\
        And turn our tears and woe to bliss; \\
        Our God is love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 164}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.7. ALLELUIA PERENNE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SING hallelujah forth in beauteous praise, \\
        O citizens of heaven, and sweetly raise \\
        An endless hallelujah.

    \item For Christ the Lord has died our souls to save; \\
        We'll sing to Him, who triumphed o'er the grave, \\
        An endless hallelujah.

    \item His mighty love doth make our hearts rejoice, \\
        And thus we render Him, with thankful voice, \\
        An endless hallelujah.

    \item When we have reached at length the future bliss, \\
        Victorious ones, our chant shall still be this, \\
        An endless hallelujah.

    \item Th' almighty triune God of love we'll praise, \\
        And utter, in untiring, thankful lays, \\
        An endless hallelujah.

    \item To Father, Son and Spirit we shall sing, \\
        Glory for evermore. To God we'll bring \\
        An endless hallelujah. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 165}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.7. PRAISE, MY SOUL}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item PRAISE, my soul, the King of heav\`en, \\
        To His feet thy tribute bring; \\
        Ransomed, healed, restored, forgiv\`en, \\
        Who like thee His praise should sing? \\
        Praise Him! Praise Him! \\
        Praise the everlasting King.

    \item Praise Him for His grace and favour \\
        To our fathers in distress; \\
        Praise Him, still the same for ever, \\
        Slow to chide and swift to bless. \\
        Praise Him! Praise Him! \\
        Glorious in His faithfulness.

    \item Father-like, He tends and spares us, \\
        Well our feeble frame He knows; \\
        In His hands He gently bears us, \\
        Rescues us from all our foes. \\
        Praise Him! Praise Him! \\
        Widely as His mercy flows.

    \item Angels, evermore adore Him, \\
        Ye behold Him face to face, \\
        Sun and moon, bow down before Him, \\
        Dwellers all in time and space. \\
        Praise Him! Praise Him! \\
        Praise with us the God of grace.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 166}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. BALLERMA}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item I WAITED for the Lord my God, \\
        And patiently did bear; \\
        At length to me He did incline, \\
        My voice and cry to hear.

    \item He took me from a fearful pit \\
        And from the miry clay, \\
        And on a rock He set my feet, \\
        Establishing my way.

    \item He put a new song in my mouth, \\
        Our God to magnify; \\
        Many shall see it and shall fear, \\
        And on the Lord rely.

    \item Oh, bless\`ed is the man whose trust \\
        Upon the Lord relies, \\
        Respecting not the proud, nor such \\
        As turn aside to lies.

    \item O Lord my God, full many are \\
        The wonders Thou hast done; \\
        Thy gracious thoughts to usward far \\
        Above all thoughts are gone.

    \item Thy tender mercies, Lord, from me \\
        Oh, do Thou not restrain; \\
        Thy loving-kindness and Thy truth, \\
        Let them me still maintain.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 167}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M. SHIRLAND}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GOD everywhere hath sway, \\
        And all things serve His might; \\
        His every act pure blessing is, \\
        His path unsullied light.

    \item He hath made bare His arm, \\
        Who shall His work withstand? \\
        'Tis He His people's cause defends, \\
        Who then shall stay His hand?

    \item We comprehend Him not; \\
        Yet earth and heav\`en tell, \\
        God sits as Sovereign on the throne, \\
        And ruleth all things well.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 168}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. D. ADORATION}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item COME, Thou fount of every blessing, \\
        Tune my heart to sing Thy praise; \\
        Streams of mercy never ceasing \\
        Call for ceaseless songs of praise. \\
        Teach me, Lord, some rapturous measure, \\
        Meet for blood-bought hosts above, \\
        While I sing the countless treasure \\
        Of my God's unchanging love.

    \item Jesus sought me when a stranger, \\
        Wandering from the fold of God; \\
        He, to rescue me from danger, \\
        Interposed His precious blood. \\
        Oh, to grace how great a debtor \\
        Daily I'm constrained to be! \\
        Let that grace, Lord, like a fetter \\
        Bind my wandering heart to Thee.

    \item Prone to wander, Lord, I know it, \\
        Prone to leave the God I love. \\
        Keep my heart from wandering, keep it \\
        Till I'm perfected above. \\
        Here I raise my Ebenezer, \\
        Hither by Thy help I'm come; \\
        Teach me, Lord, to do Thy pleasure, \\
        Till I reach my heavenly home.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 169}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.10.10. PEEL CASTLE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item IN safety standing on the Red Sea's shore, \\
        The sons of Israel viewed the closing grave \\
        Of groaning tyranny and bondage sore, \\
        As Egypt's hosts sank 'neath the surging wave.

    \item Long, weary years had Pharaoh's jealous might, \\
        With unrelenting rigour, crushed them low; \\
        But God Almighty bared His arm in fight, \\
        And to destruction hurled the haughty foe.

    \item Saved now and free! oh, soul-inspiring thought! \\
        Well might ascend from Israel's ransomed throng \\
        To Him whose works had their salvation wrought, \\
        Deep thanks expressed in loud and rapturous song.

    \item Ten, tens of thousands sang the matchless fame \\
        Of their Deliverer in triumphant lays, \\
        Proclaiming far Jehovah's glorious name \\
        As truly worthy of eternal praise.

    \item So let us sing, for greater cause have we \\
        Than Israel had to praise the living God. \\
        From earthly tyrant's bonds were they set free--- \\
        We groaned in sin, 'neath Satan's despot rod.

    \item Let joyous songs from thankful hearts ascend \\
        To God our Saviour, who, from light above, \\
        Did in His Son a great Deliverer send, \\
        Who freed us by His work of mighty love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 170}}
    \\
    \textit{S.M.D. DIADEMATA}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item OUR times are in Thy hand. \\
        O God, we wish them there; \\
        Our lives, our souls, our all, we leave \\
        Entirely to Thy care. \\
        Our times are in Thy hand, \\
        Whatever they may be; \\
        Pleasing or painful, dark or bright, \\
        As best may seem to Thee.

    \item Our times are in Thy hand. \\
        Why should we doubt or fear? \\
        Our Father's hand will never cause \\
        His child a needless tear. \\
        Our times are in Thy hand, \\
        Jesus, the Crucified; \\
        The hand our many sins have pierced \\
        Is now our guard and guide.

    \item Our times are in Thy hand, \\
        Jesus, the Advocate, \\
        Nor can that hand be stretched in vain, \\
        For us to supplicate. \\
        Our times are in Thy hand; \\
        We'd always trust in Thee, \\
        Till we, in yonder heavenly land, \\
        Thyself in glory see.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 171}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. ST. ANN}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O GOD, our help in ages past, \\
        Our hope for time to come, \\
        Our shelter from the stormy blast, \\
        And our eternal home---

    \item Under the shadow of Thy throne \\
        Thy saints have dwelt secure; \\
        Sufficient is Thine arm alone, \\
        And our defence is sure.

    \item Before the hills in order stood \\
        Or earth received her frame, \\
        From everlasting Thou art God, \\
        To endless years the same.

    \item Thou turnest human flesh to dust; \\
        "Return, ye sons of men." \\
        All men are made from dust at first, \\
        And turn to dust again.

    \item A thousand ages in Thy sight \\
        Are like an evening gone; \\
        Short, as the watch that ends the night \\
        Before the rising sun.

    \item The busy tribes of flesh and blood, \\
        With all their joys and cares, \\
        Are carried downwards by a flood \\
        And perish unawares.

    \item Time, like an ever-rolling stream, \\
        Bears all its sons away; \\
        They fly forgotten, as a dream \\
        Dies at the opening day.

    \item Like flowery fields the nations stand \\
        Pleased with the morning light; \\
        The flowers, beneath the mower's hand \\
        Lie withering ere 'tis night.

    \item O God, our help in ages past, \\
        Our hope for time to come, \\
        Be Thou our guard while life shall last, \\
        And our eternal home. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 172}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. WILTSHIRE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O THOU, whose bounty fills our cup \\
        With every blessing meet, \\
        We give Thee thanks for every drop, \\
        The bitter and the sweet.

    \item We thank Thee for the desert road, \\
        And for the riverside; \\
        For all Thy goodness has bestowed, \\
        And all Thy grace denied.

    \item We thank Thee for the smile and frown, \\
        And for the gain and loss; \\
        We bless Thee for the future crown, \\
        And for the present cross.

    \item We bless Thee for the glad increase, \\
        And for the waning joy; \\
        And for the calm and settled peace \\
        That nothing can destroy.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 173}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. ORLINGTON}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HAST thou not known, hast thou not heard, \\
        That firm remains on high \\
        The everlasting throne of Him \\
        Who formed the earth and sky?

    \item Art thou afraid His power shall fail \\
        When comes thine evil day? \\
        And can an all-creating arm \\
        Grow weary or decay?

    \item Supreme in wisdom as in power \\
        The Rock of ages stands, \\
        Though Him thou canst not see, nor trace \\
        The working of His hands.

    \item He gives the conquest to the weak, \\
        Supports the fainting heart; \\
        And courage in the evil hour \\
        His heavenly aids impart.

    \item Mere human power shall fast decay, \\
        And youthful vigour cease; \\
        But they who wait upon the Lord \\
        In strength shall still increase.

    \item They with unwearied feet shall tread \\
        The path of life divine, \\
        With growing ardour onward move, \\
        With growing brightness shine.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 174}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M.D. OLD 29TH.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HE sitteth o'er the waterfloods, \\
        And He is strong to save; \\
        He sitteth o'er the waterfloods, \\
        And guides each drifting wave. \\
        Though loud around the vessel's prow \\
        The waves may toss and break, \\
        Yet, at His word, they sink to rest, \\
        As on a tranquil lake.

    \item He sitteth o'er the waterfloods, \\
        When waves of sorrow rise; \\
        And while He holds the bitter cup \\
        He wipes the tearful eyes. \\
        He knows how long the wilful heart \\
        Requires the chastening grief, \\
        And soon as sorrow's work is done \\
        'Tis He who sends relief.

    \item He sitteth o'er the waterfloods, \\
        As in the days of old, \\
        When o'er the Saviour's sinless head \\
        The waves and billows rolled. \\
        Yea, all the billows passed o'er Him; \\
        Our sins they bore Him down; \\
        For us He met the crushing storm, \\
        He met th' Almighty's frown.

    \item He sitteth o'er the waterfloods, \\
        Then doubt and fear no more; \\
        For He who passed through all the storms \\
        Has reached the heavenly shore. \\
        And every tempest-driven bark, \\
        With Jesus for its guide, \\
        Will soon be moored in harbour calm, \\
        In glory to abide. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 175}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.8.8.7. SEBASTIAN}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SING praise to God who reigns above, \\
        The God of all creation, \\
        The God of power, the God of love, \\
        The God of our salvation. \\
        With healing balm our souls He fills, \\
        And every faithless murmur stills; \\
        To God all praise and glory.

    \item The angel-hosts, O King of kings, \\
        Thy praise for ever telling, \\
        In earth and sky all living things \\
        Beneath Thy shadow dwelling, \\
        Adore the wisdom which could span, \\
        And power which formed creation's plan; \\
        To God all praise and glory.

    \item What God's almighty power hath made \\
        His gracious mercy keepeth. \\
        By morning glow or evening shade \\
        His watchful eye ne'er sleepeth. \\
        Within the kingdom of His might, \\
        Lo, all is just, and all is right; \\
        To God all praise and glory.

    \item O ye who bear Christ's holy name \\
        Give God all power and glory; \\
        All ye who own His power proclaim \\
        And sing the wondrous story. \\
        Cast each false idol from its throne, \\
        The Lord is God and He alone; \\
        To God all praise and glory.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 176}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. EASTGATE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GOD moves in a mysterious way, \\
        His wonders to perform; \\
        He plants His footsteps in the sea, \\
        And rides upon the storm.

    \item Deep in unfathomable mines \\
        Of never-failing skill, \\
        He treasures up His bright designs, \\
        And works His sovereign will.

    \item Ye fearful saints, fresh courage take; \\
        The clouds ye so much dread \\
        Are big with mercy, and shall break \\
        In blessings on your head.

    \item Judge not the Lord by feeble sense, \\
        But trust Him for His grace; \\
        Behind a frowning providence \\
        He hides a smiling face.

    \item His purposes will ripen fast, \\
        Unfolding every hour; \\
        The bud may have a bitter taste, \\
        But sweet will be the flower.

    \item Blind unbelief is sure to err, \\
        And scan His work in vain; \\
        God is His own interpreter, \\
        And He will make it plain.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 177}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. CASTLE STREET}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item AS sinners saved we gladly praise \\
        The Author of redeeming grace; \\
        Father, 'tis Thine almighty power \\
        Secures us when the tempests lower.

    \item Thy love's a refuge ever nigh, \\
        Thy watchfulness a mountain high; \\
        Thy name a rock which winds above \\
        And waves below can never move.

    \item Thy faithfulness for ever sure \\
        For endless ages shall endure; \\
        Thy perfect work shall ever prove \\
        The depth of Thine unchanging love.

    \item While all things change Thou changest not; \\
        Forgetting ne'er, though oft forgot; \\
        Thy love eternally the same \\
        Is as enduring as Thy name.

    \item Lord, we would then rejoice and praise \\
        The source of all this wondrous grace. \\
        Father, Thine everlasting power \\
        Will keep us safe in danger's hour.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 178}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. SOME TIME}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Slow]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item NOT now, but in the coming years, \\
        It may be in the better land, \\
        We'll read the meaning of our tears, \\
        And there, some time, we'll understand.

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{Then trust in God through all thy days;} \\
    \textit{Fear not, for He doth hold thy hand;} \\
    \textit{Though dark thy way, still sing and praise;} \\
    \textit{Some time, some time we'll understand.}
    \vspace{0.4em}

    \item We'll catch the broken threads again, \\
        And finish what we here began; \\
        Heaven will the myst\`eries explain, \\
        And then, ah then, we'll understand.

    \item We'll know why clouds instead of sun \\
        Were over many a cherished plan; \\
        Why song has ceased when scarce begun; \\
        'Tis there, some time, we'll understand.

    \item Why what we long for most of all \\
        Eludes so oft our eager hand, \\
        Why hopes are crushed, and castles fall, \\
        Up there, some time, we'll understand.

    \item God knows the way, He holds the key, \\
        He guides us with unerring hand; \\
        Some time with tearless eyes we'll see; \\
        Yes there, up there, we'll understand.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 179}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. SAFE HOME}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item JEHOVAH is our strength, \\
        And He shall be our song; \\
        We shall o'ercome at length, \\
        Although our foes be strong; \\
        In vain doth Satan then oppose, \\
        For God is stronger than His foes.

    \item The Lord our refuge is, \\
        And ever will remain; \\
        Since He hath made us His \\
        He will our cause maintain; \\
        In vain our enemies oppose, \\
        For God is stronger than His foes.

    \item The Lord our portion is; \\
        What can we wish for more? \\
        As long as we are His \\
        We never can be poor; \\
        In vain do earth and hell oppose, \\
        For God is stronger than His foes.

    \item The Lord our Shepherd is, \\
        He knows our every need; \\
        And since we now are His, \\
        His care our souls will feed; \\
        In vain do sin and death oppose, \\
        For God is stronger than His foes.

    \item This God our Father is, \\
        Our names are on His heart; \\
        We ever shall be His, \\
        He ne'er from us will part; \\
        In vain the world and flesh oppose, \\
        For God is stronger than His foes.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 180}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. BENTLEY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SOMETIMES a light surprises \\
        The Christian while he sings; \\
        It is the Lord who rises \\
        With healing in His wings. \\
        When comforts are declining, \\
        He grants the soul again \\
        A season of clear shining, \\
        To cheer it after rain.

    \item In holy contemplation \\
        We gladly then pursue \\
        The theme of God's salvation, \\
        And find it ever new. \\
        Set free from present sorrow \\
        We cheerfully can say, \\
        E'en let th' unknown to-morrow \\
        Bring with it what it may;

    \item It can bring with it nothing \\
        But He will bear us through; \\
        Who gives the lilies clothing \\
        Will clothe His people too. \\
        Beneath the spreading heavens \\
        No creature but is fed; \\
        And He who feeds the ravens \\
        Will give His children bread.

    \item Though vine or fig-tree neither \\
        Their wonted fruit shall bear; \\
        Though all the field shall wither, \\
        Nor flocks nor herds be there; \\
        Yet God the same abiding, \\
        His praise shall tune my voice; \\
        For while in Him confiding, \\
        I cannot but rejoice.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 181}}
    \\
    \textit{8.4.8.4.8.8.8.4. MAIN}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THROUGH the love of God our Saviour \\
        All will be well; \\
        Free and changeless is His favour, \\
        All will be well; \\
        Precious is the blood that healed us, \\
        Perfect is the grace that sealed us, \\
        Strong the hand stretched forth to shield us, \\
        All will be well.

    \item Though we pass through tribulation, \\
        All will be well; \\
        Ours is such a full salvation, \\
        All, all is well; \\
        Happy still in God confiding, \\
        Fruitful if in Christ abiding, \\
        Holy through the Spirit's guiding, \\
        All must be well.

    \item We expect a bright tomorrow, \\
        All will be well; \\
        Faith can sing through days of sorrow, \\
        All, all is well; \\
        On our Father's love relying, \\
        He our every need supplying, \\
        Or in living or in dying, \\
        All must be well.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 182}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.11.11. HOUGHTON}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THOUGH troubles assail and dangers affright, \\
        Though friends should all fail and foes all unite; \\
        Yet one thing secures us, whatever betide--- \\
        The Scripture assures us, "The Lord will provide."

    \item The birds, without barn or storehouse, are fed; \\
        From them let us learn to trust for our bread. \\
        His saints what is fitting shall ne'er be denied, \\
        So long as 'tis written, "The Lord will provide."

    \item His call to obey, like Abram of old \\
        Not knowing our way, but faith makes us bold; \\
        For though we are strangers we have a good guide, \\
        And trust in all dangers; "The Lord will provide."

    \item No strength of our own or goodness we claim; \\
        Yet since we have known the Saviour's great name, \\
        In this our strong tow\`er for safety we hide, \\
        Almighty His power; "The Lord will provide."

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 183}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.11.11. HOUGHTON}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BEGONE unbelief, the Saviour is near, \\
        And for my relief will surely appear. \\
        By prayer let me wrestle, and He will perform; \\
        With Christ in the vessel, I smile at the storm.

    \item Though dark be the way, since He is my Guide, \\
        'Tis mine to obey, 'tis His to provide. \\
        Though cisterns be broken and creatures all fail, \\
        The word He hath spoken shall surely prevail.

    \item His love in times past forbids me to think \\
        He'll leave me at last in trouble to sink; \\
        Each sweet Ebenezer I pass in review \\
        Confirms His good pleasure to help me right through.

    \item Why should I complain of want or distress, \\
        Temptation or pain? He told me no less. \\
        The heirs of salvation, I know from His word, \\
        Through much tribulation must follow their Lord.

    \item How bitter that cup no heart can conceive, \\
        The Saviour drank up, that sinners might live; \\
        His way was much rougher and darker than mine; \\
        Did Christ my Lord suffer, and shall I repine?

    \item Since all that I meet shall work for my good, \\
        The bitter is sweet, the medicine is food; \\
        Though painful at present 'twill cease before long, \\
        And then, oh how pleasant the conqu\`error's song!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 184}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. HOLY TRINITY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item I BOW me to Thy will, O God, \\
        And all Thy ways adore; \\
        And every day I live I'd seek \\
        To please Thee more and more.

    \item Why should I care, O bless\`ed God, \\
        Since all my cares are Thine? \\
        Why not in triumph live, since Thou \\
        Hast made Thy triumphs mine?

    \item Lead on, lead on triumphantly, \\
        O bless\`ed Lord, lead on; \\
        Faith's pilgrim sons behind Thee seek \\
        The road that Thou hast gone.

    \item He always wins who sides with God, \\
        To him no chance is lost; \\
        God's will is sweetest to him when \\
        It triumphs at his cost.

    \item Ill that God blesses is our good, \\
        And unblest good is ill; \\
        And all is right that seems most wrong \\
        If, Lord, it be Thy will. Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 185}}
    \\
    \textit{L.M. NICOMACHUS}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GOD of my life, to Thee I call; \\
        Afflicted, at Thy feet I fall. \\
        When the great waterfloods prevail, \\
        Leave not my trembling heart to fail.

    \item Friend of the friendless and the faint, \\
        Where should I lodge my deep complaint, \\
        Where, but with Thee, whose open door \\
        Invites the helpless and the poor?

    \item Did ever mourner plead with Thee, \\
        And Thou refuse that mourner's plea? \\
        Does not the word still fixed remain, \\
        That none shall seek Thy face in vain?

    \item That were a grief I could not bear, \\
        Didst Thou not hear and answer prayer. \\
        But a prayer-hearing, answering God \\
        Supports me under every load.

    \item Fair is the lot that's cast for me; \\
        I have an Advocate with Thee. \\
        They whom the world caresses most \\
        Have no such privilege to boast.

    \item Poor though I am, despised, forgot, \\
        Yet God, my God, forgets me not; \\
        And he is safe, and must succeed, \\
        For whom the Lord vouchsafes to plead.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 186}}
    \\
    \textit{6.6.6.6.8.8. LENOX}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O BLESS\`ED God, how kind \\
        Are all Thy ways to me, \\
        Whose dark, benighted mind \\
        Was enmity to Thee! \\
        Yet now, subdued by sovereign grace, \\
        My spirit longs for Thine embrace.

    \item How precious are Thy thoughts \\
        That o'er my spirit roll! \\
        They swell beyond my faults \\
        And captivate my soul. \\
        How great their sum, how high they rise, \\
        Can ne'er be known beneath the skies.

    \item Preserved by Jesus, when \\
        My feet made haste to hell, \\
        And there should I have gone, \\
        But Thou doest all things well. \\
        Thy love was great, Thy mercy free, \\
        Which from the pit delivered me.

    \item Before Thy hands had made \\
        The sun to rule the day, \\
        Or earth's foundation laid, \\
        Or fashioned Adam's clay, \\
        What thoughts of peace and mercy flowed \\
        In Thy great heart of love, O God!

    \item A monument of grace, \\
        A sinner saved by blood, \\
        The streams of love I trace \\
        Up to the fountain, God; \\
        And in His sovereign counsels see \\
        Eternal thoughts of love to me.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 187}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.10.10.10.10. FINLANDIA.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BE still, my soul, the Lord is on thy side; \\
        Bear patiently the cross of grief or pain. \\
        Leave to thy God to order and provide; \\
        In every change He faithful will remain. \\
        Be still, my soul, thy heavenly Friend \\
        Through thorny ways leads to a joyful end.

    \item Be still, my soul, thy God doth undertake \\
        To guide the future as He has the past. \\
        Thy hope, thy confidence let nothing shake; \\
        All now mysterious shall be bright at last. \\
        Be still, my soul, the waves and winds still know \\
        His voice, who ruled them while He dwelt below.

    \item Be still, my soul, the hour is hastening on \\
        When we shall be for ever with the Lord; \\
        When disappointment, grief and fear are gone, \\
        Sorrow forgot, love's purest joys restored. \\
        Be still, my soul, when change and tears are past, \\
        All safe and bless\`ed we shall meet at last.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 188}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. with Chorus HE WILL HIDE ME.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WHEN the storms of life are raging, \\
        Tempests wild on sea and land, \\
        I will seek a place of refuge \\
        In the shadow of God's hand.

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{He will hide me, He will hide me,} \\
    \textit{Where no harm can e'er betide me;} \\
    \textit{He will hide me, safely hide me,} \\
    \textit{In the shadow of His hand.}
    \vspace{0.4em}

    \item Though He may send some affliction, \\
        'Twill but make me long for home; \\
        For in love and not in anger, \\
        All His chast\`enings will come.

    \item Enemies may strive to injure, \\
        Satan all his arts employ; \\
        God will turn what seems to harm me \\
        Into everlasting joy.

    \item So, while here the cross I'm bearing, \\
        Meeting storms and billows wild, \\
        Jesus for my soul is caring, \\
        Nought can harm His Father's child.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 189}}
    \\
    \textit{8.4.8.4.8.8.8.4. TEMPLE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GOD'S almighty arms are round me, \\
        Peace, peace is mine. \\
        Judgment scenes need not confound me, \\
        Peace, peace is mine. \\
        Jesus came Himself and sought me, \\
        Sold to death, He found and bought me, \\
        Then my bless\`ed freedom taught me; \\
        Peace, peace is mine.

    \item While I hear life's surging billows, \\
        Peace, peace is mine. \\
        Why suspend my harp on willows? \\
        Peace, peace is mine. \\
        I may sing with Christ beside me \\
        Though a thousand ills betide me, \\
        Safely He hath sworn to guide me; \\
        Peace, peace is mine.

    \item Every trial draws Him nearer, \\
        Peace, peace is mine. \\
        All His strokes but make Him dearer, \\
        Peace, peace is mine. \\
        Bless I then the hand that smiteth \\
        Gently, and to heal delighteth; \\
        'Tis against my sins He fighteth, \\
        Peace, peace is mine.

    \item Welcome, every rising sunlight, \\
        Peace, peace is mine. \\
        Nearer home each rolling midnight, \\
        Peace, peace is mine. \\
        Death and hell cannot appal me, \\
        Safe in Christ whate'er befall me, \\
        Calmly wait I till He call me; \\
        Peace, peace is mine.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 190}}
    \\
    \textit{8.4.8.8.4. GOD HOLDS THE KEY.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GOD holds the key of all unknown, \\
        And I am glad; \\
        If other hands should hold the key, \\
        Or if He trusted it to me, \\
        I might be sad.

    \item What if tomorrow's cares were here, \\
        Without its rest! \\
        I'd rather He unlock the day, \\
        And, as the hours swing open, say, \\
        "My will is best."

    \item The very dimness of my sight \\
        Makes me secure; \\
        For, groping in my misty way, \\
        I feel His hand, I hear Him say, \\
        "My help is sure."

    \item I cannot read His future plans, \\
        But this I know--- \\
        I have the smiling of His face, \\
        And all the refuge of His grace, \\
        While here below.

    \item Enough! this covers all my wants, \\
        And so I rest. \\
        For what I cannot, He can see, \\
        And in His care I saved shall be, \\
        For ever blest.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 191}}
    \\
    \textit{8.8.8.4. MEMORIA.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item MY God, my Father, while I stray \\
        Far from my home on life's rough way, \\
        Oh teach me from my heart to say, \\
        "Thy will be done."

    \item Though dark my path or sad my lot, \\
        Let me be still, and murmur not, \\
        But breathe the prayer divinely taught, \\
        "Thy will be done."

    \item What though in lonely grief I sigh \\
        For friends beloved, no longer nigh? \\
        Submissive still would I reply, \\
        "Thy will be done."

    \item If Thou shouldst call me to resign \\
        What most I prize, it ne'er was mine; \\
        I only yield Thee what is Thine; \\
        Thy will be done.

    \item If but my fainting heart be blest \\
        With Thy good Spirit for my Guest, \\
        My God, to Thee I'll leave the rest; \\
        Thy will be done.

    \item Renew my will from day to day, \\
        Blend it with Thine, and take away \\
        All that now makes it hard to say, \\
        "Thy will be done."

    \item And when on earth I breathe no more \\
        The prayer oft mixed with tears before, \\
        I'll sing upon a happier shore, \\
        "Thy will be done." Amen.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 192}}
    \\
    \textit{4.6.8.8.4. PATIENCE.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item KEPT, safely kept, \\
        My fears away are swept; \\
        In weakness to my God I cling, \\
        Though foes be strong I calmly sing, \\
        Kept, safely kept.

    \item Kept by His power, \\
        Whatever dangers lower; \\
        The strength of God's almighty arm \\
        Doth shield my soul from every harm; \\
        Kept by His power.

    \item Through simple faith, \\
        Believing what He saith, \\
        Unshaken on my God I lean, \\
        And realise His power unseen, \\
        But known to faith.

    \item Kept all the way, \\
        E'en to salvation's day; \\
        His mighty love ne'er cold shall wax, \\
        Nor shall His powerful grasp relax, \\
        Through all the way.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 193}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7.4.7. BENEDICTION.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item I WILL never, never leave thee, \\
        I will never thee forsake; \\
        I will guard and save and keep thee \\
        For My name and mercy's sake. \\
        Fear no evil, \\
        Only all My counsel take.

    \item When the storm is raging round thee, \\
        Call on Me in humble prayer; \\
        I will fold My arms about thee, \\
        Guard thee with the tenderest care. \\
        In the trial \\
        I will make thy pathway clear.

    \item When the sky above is glowing, \\
        And around thee all is bright, \\
        Pleasure like a river flowing, \\
        All things tending to delight, \\
        I'll be with thee, \\
        I will guide thy steps aright.

    \item When thy soul is dark and clouded, \\
        Filled with doubt and grief and care, \\
        Through the mist by which 'tis shrouded \\
        I will make a light appear, \\
        And the banner \\
        Of My love I will uprear.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 194}}
    \\
    \textit{11.11.11.11. ST. DENIO}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item HOW firm a foundation, ye saints of the Lord, \\
        Is laid for your faith in His excellent word! \\
        What more can He say, than to you He hath said, \\
        You who unto Jesus for refuge have fled?

    \item "In every condition, in sickness, in health, \\
        In poverty's vale or abounding in wealth, \\
        At home or abroad, on the land or the sea, \\
        As th' day may demand shall thy strength ever be.

    \item "Fear not, I am with thee, oh, be not dismayed, \\
        I, I am thy God, and will still give thee aid; \\
        I'll strengthen thee, help thee, and cause thee to stand, \\
        Upheld by My righteous omnipotent hand.

    \item "When through the deep waters I call thee to go, \\
        The floods of distress shall not thee overflow; \\
        For I will be with thee, thy troubles to bless, \\
        And sanctify to thee thy deepest distress.

    \item "When through fiery trials thy pathway shall lie, \\
        My grace all-sufficient shall be thy supply; \\
        The flame shall not hurt thee; I only design \\
        Thy dross to consume and thy gold to refine.

    \item "E'en down to old age all My people shall prove \\
        My sovereign, eternal, unchangeable love; \\
        And when hoary hairs shall their temples adorn, \\
        Like lambs they shall still on My bosom be borne.

    \item "The soul that on Jesus hath leaned for repose, \\
        I will not, I cannot desert to its foes; \\
        That soul, though all hell should endeavour to shake, \\
        I'll never, no never, no never forsake."

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 195}}
    \\
    \textit{7.6.7.6. D. ANGELS' STORY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THE Lord thy God is with thee, \\
        Where'er thy pathway lie, \\
        In summer's shining hours, \\
        'neath dull winter's sky. \\
        In all thy joys and blessings \\
        His hand has its full share; \\
        Whilst oft-times in thy sorrow, \\
        His purposes lie there.

    \item The Lord thy God is with thee, \\
        He seeth every tear; \\
        Thy weight of grief He knoweth, \\
        Thou still to Him art dear. \\
        Thy prayers, e'en though unuttered, \\
        Pent in thine aching heart, \\
        The Lord thy God doth hear them, \\
        And with thee bears a part.

    \item The Lord thy God is with thee, \\
        Although thy way seem hard; \\
        In keeping His commandments \\
        Thine is a sure reward. \\
        Though called to suffer hardship \\
        In fighting for His name, \\
        The Lord thy God is with thee, \\
        His promise still the same.

    \item The Lord thy God is with thee, \\
        O saint, do not despair; \\
        His mighty arm is round thee; \\
        Confide in Him thy care. \\
        If e'er thou growest weary \\
        And feel'st how weak thy strength, \\
        Forget not He is with thee; \\
        Thou must o'ercome at length.

    \item The Lord thy God is with thee; \\
        Then bright shall be the dawn \\
        Of day that bringeth victory, \\
        The fruit of sorrows borne. \\
        And looking o'er thy pathway, \\
        With joy thou then wilt say, \\
        "The Lord my God was with me \\
        Each moment of the day."

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 196}}
    \\
    \textit{8.5.8.5. D. SONG OF THE BRIDE.}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item FEAR thou not, for He is with thee; \\
        Be thou not dismayed. \\
        Look to Him, He'll guide and shield thee; \\
        Do not be afraid. \\
        When temptations round thee gather \\
        And thy strength seems small, \\
        Look above to God, thy Helper; \\
        Find in Him thine all.

    \item He has promised to be with thee, \\
        Hold thee by thy hand; \\
        Why not then go forth with courage \\
        At thy Lord's command? \\
        Knowing that the way He leadeth \\
        Must be always best, \\
        Fear thou not, and do not falter; \\
        On His promise rest.

    \item Cast on Him thine every burden, \\
        Whatsoe'er it be; \\
        Hear His voice unto thee saying, \\
        "Cast thy care on Me." \\
        He has said, "I'll never leave thee, \\
        Never thee forsake;" \\
        Cast then all thy care upon Him, \\
        And fresh courage take.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 197}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. D. ONLY WAITING}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item "ABBA, Father," Lord, we call Thee, \\
        Hallowed name from day to day. \\
        'Tis Thy children's right to know Thee, \\
        None but children "Abba" say. \\
        This high glory we inherit, \\
        Thy free gift through Jesus' blood; \\
        God the Spirit with our spirit \\
        Witnesseth we're sons of God.

    \item Though our nature's fall in Adam \\
        Shut us wholly out from God, \\
        Thus it was His counsel brought us \\
        Nearer still through Jesus' blood. \\
        For in Him we found redemption, \\
        Grace, and glory in the Son. \\
        Oh, the height and depth of mercy, \\
        Christ and we through grace are one!

    \item Richest stores of heavenly blessings \\
        God hath given through Christ, His Son; \\
        With the Holy Spirit's power \\
        Safe to lead His children on. \\
        Abba, Father, makes all certain, \\
        Both by word and oath and blood; \\
        Abba saith, "They are My people," \\
        And they say, "He is our God."

    \item Hence through all the changing seasons, \\
        Trouble, sorrow, sickness, woe, \\
        Nothing changeth God's affections, \\
        Abba's love shall bring us through. \\
        Soon shall all the blood-bought children \\
        Round the throne their anthems raise, \\
        And in songs of rich salvation \\
        Shout to Abba endless praise.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 198}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. D. DEERHURST}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item ABBA, Father, we approach Thee \\
        In our Saviour's precious name; \\
        We Thy children, here assembling, \\
        Now Thy promised blessing claim. \\
        From our sins His blood has freed us, \\
        'Tis through Him we now draw nigh; \\
        And Thy Spirit, too, has taught us, \\
        "Abba, Father," thus to cry.

    \item Once as prodigals we wandered \\
        In our folly far from Thee; \\
        But Thy grace o'er sin abounding \\
        Rescued us from mis\`ery. \\
        Thou Thy prodigals hast pardoned, \\
        Kissed us with a father's love, \\
        Killed the fatted calf, and called us \\
        E'er to dwell with Thee above.

    \item Clothed in garments of salvation, \\
        Given the children's favoured place, \\
        We rejoice and Thou rejoicest \\
        In the riches of Thy grace. \\
        Abba, Father, all adore Thee, \\
        All rejoice in heaven above, \\
        While in us they learn the wonders \\
        Of Thy wisdom, power and love.

    \item Soon before Thy throne assembled, \\
        All Thy children shall proclaim--- \\
        "Glory, everlasting glory \\
        Be to God and to the Lamb." \\
        Abba, then we'll cry, exulting, \\
        In that holy, happy place, \\
        Thine eternal love beholding, \\
        Mirrored in the Saviour's face.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 199}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.10.10.10.10. NOMEN DOMINI}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GOD the Unsearchable, mighty and great, \\
        Thou, who the heavens and the earth didst create, \\
        Known to the Hebrews as true Elohim, \\
        Worshipped, adored by six-winged seraphim; \\
        Christ bore our sins when He died on the tree, \\
        Bringing us nigh by His death unto Thee.

    \item Timeless and endless, th'eternal I AM, \\
        Infinite, holy; Jehovah Thy name; \\
        Glorious name of th'Ineffable Three, \\
        Changeless Jehovah we bow before Thee. \\
        Father and Spirit and Son evermore, \\
        Triune Jehovah we humbly adore.

    \item Rich, all possessing, our Lord, Adonai, \\
        Owner of earth and of sea and of sky, \\
        Ever and ever Thy name be adored, \\
        Wealthy possessor of all things, our Lord. \\
        Christ, who redeemed us from sin and the Fall, \\
        Taught us the Lord as our Father to call.

    \item Jah is Thy wondrous, mysterious name; \\
        By it we hail Thee, Thy glory acclaim. \\
        Jah is Thy name, O Thou Ancient of days, \\
        Solemnly sing we Thine infinite praise. \\
        Glorious the knowledge of fatherly love \\
        Brought to our hearts by the heavenly Dove.

    \item El, Thou Almighty, Thou powerful and strong, \\
        (Strength for Thy people the whole way along); \\
        Humbly we bow and acknowledge Thy claim, \\
        Mighty unsearchable God is Thy name. \\
        Christ, who redeemed us from sin and the Fall, \\
        Taught us this God as our Father to call.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 200}}
    \\
    \textit{C.M. BEHOLD, WHAT LOVE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BEHOLD what love, what boundless love, \\
        The Father hath bestowed \\
        On sinners lost, that we should be \\
        Now called the sons of God!

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{Behold what manner of love the Father hath bestowed upon us,} \\
    \textit{That we should be called the sons of God!}
    \vspace{0.4em}

    \item No longer far from Him, but now \\
        By precious blood made nigh, \\
        Accepted in the Well-beloved, \\
        Near to God's heart we lie.

    \item What we in glory soon shall be, \\
        It doth not yet appear; \\
        But when our bless\`ed Lord we see, \\
        We shall His image bear.

    \item With such a bless\`ed hope in view, \\
        We would more holy be, \\
        More like our risen, glorious Lord \\
        Whose face we shall soon see.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 201}}
    \\
    \textit{Irregular GREAT IS THY FAITHFULNESS}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GREAT is Thy faithfulness, O God my Father, \\
        There is no shadow of turning with Thee; \\
        Thou changest not; Thy compassions, they fail not; \\
        As Thou hast been Thou for ever wilt be.

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{Great is Thy faithfulness!} \\
    \textit{Great is Thy faithfulness!} \\
    \textit{Morning by morning new mercies I see.} \\
    \textit{All I have needed Thy hand hath provided;} \\
    \textit{Great is Thy faithfulness, Lord, unto me!}
    \vspace{0.4em}

    \item Summer and winter and springtime and harvest, \\
        Sun, moon and stars in their courses above, \\
        Join with all Nature in manifold witness \\
        To Thy great faithfulness, mercy and love.

    \item Pardon for sin and a peace that endureth, \\
        Thine own dear presence to cheer and to guide; \\
        Strength for today and bright hope for tomorrow, \\
        Blessings all mine, with ten thousand beside!

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 202}}
    \\
    \textit{10.10.10.10. ELLERS}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item TRUST not in man, the arm of flesh will fail; \\
        Trust not in man, his strength cannot avail; \\
        Trust not in man, though great may be his boast; \\
        Trust not in man---who trusts in him is lost.

    \item Trust thou in God, though all around deride; \\
        Trust thou in God when good or ill betide; \\
        Trust thou in God, though strongest men may fall; \\
        Trust thou in God, make Him thy all in all.

    \item Trust thou in God in dark and stormy days; \\
        Trust thou in God in smooth and pleasant ways; \\
        Trust thou in God, for Satan aye is near; \\
        Trust thou in God, and then thou need'st not fear.

    \item Trust thou in God, He is a rock, a tower; \\
        Trust thou in God, thy stronghold is His power; \\
        Trust thou in God, His promises are sure; \\
        Trust thou in God, in Him thou art secure.

    \item Trust thou in God, in secret to Him pray, \\
        Trust thou in God, He'll be thy strength and stay; \\
        Trust thou in God, make Him thy dearest friend, \\
        Trust thou in God, He'll keep thee to the end.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\thispagestyle{empty}
\vspace*{3.5cm}
\begin{center}
    {\LARGE \textbf{SECTION 10}}\\[0.8em]
    {\Large \textsc{Our Lord Jesus Christ}}
\end{center}
\vspace{3.5cm}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 203}}
    \\
    \textit{C. M. ATHERTON}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item AND did the Holy and the Just, \\
        The Sovereign of the skies, \\
        Stoop down to man's estate and dust, \\
        That guilty worms might rise?

    \item Yes, the Redeemer left the throne, \\
        The radiant throne on high, \\
        (Surprising mercy, love unknown) \\
        To suffer, bleed and die.

    \item He took the rebel sinner's place, \\
        And suffered in his stead; \\
        For man, oh, miracle of grace! \\
        For sinful man He bled.

    \item What heavenly truths and wonders dwell \\
        In that atoning blood! \\
        By this are sinners saved from hell, \\
        And reconciled to God.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 204}}
    \\
    \textit{7.7. WHO IS HE?}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Slow. Softly]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WHO is He in yonder stall, \\
        At whose feet the shepherds fall?

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{'Tis the Lord, oh wondrous story!} \\
    \textit{'Tis the Lord, the King of glory!} \\
    \textit{At His feet we humbly fall,} \\
    \textit{Laud Him, own Him Lord of all.}
    \vspace{0.4em}

    \item Who is He in deep distress, \\
        Fasting in the wilderness?

    \item Who is He the people bless \\
        For His words of gentleness?

    \item Who is He to whom they bring \\
        All the sick and sorrowing?

    \item Who is He who stands and weeps \\
        At the grave where Lazarus sleeps?

    \item Who is He the gathering throng \\
        Greet with loud triumphant song?

    \item Lo at midnight, who is He \\
        Prays in dark Gethsemane?

    \item Who is He on yonder tree \\
        Dies in grief and agony?

    \item Who is He who from the grave \\
        Rises glorious, strong to save?

    \item Who is He who on the throne \\
        Sits till all His sway shall own?

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 205}}
    \\
    \textit{5.6.6.5. EXULTATE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item REJOICE and be glad! \\
        The Redeemer hath come! \\
        Go, look on His cradle, \\
        His cross and His tomb.

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{Sound His praises, tell the story} \\
    \textit{    Of Him who was slain.} \\
    \textit{Sound His praises, tell with gladness,} \\
    \textit{    He liveth again!}
    \vspace{0.4em}

    \item Rejoice and be glad! \\
        It is sunshine at last! \\
        The clouds have departed, \\
        The shadows are past.

    \item Rejoice and be glad! \\
        For the blood hath been shed; \\
        Redemption is finished, \\
        The price hath been paid.

    \item Rejoice and be glad! \\
        Now the pardon is free! \\
        The Just for the unjust \\
        Hath died on the tree.

    \item Rejoice and be glad! \\
        For the Lamb who was slain \\
        O'er death is triumphant, \\
        And liveth again.

    \item Rejoice and be glad! \\
        For the Lord is on high; \\
        Christ pleadeth for us on \\
        God's throne in the sky.

    \item Rejoice and be glad! \\
        For He cometh again; \\
        He cometh in glory, \\
        The Lamb who was slain.

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{Sound His praises, tell the story} \\
    \textit{    Of Him who was slain.} \\
    \textit{Sound His praises, tell with gladness,} \\
    \textit{    He cometh again!}
    \vspace{0.4em}

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 206}}
    \\
    \textit{8.5.8.5. D. SONG OF THE BRIDE}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item GENTLENESS and tender feeling, \\
            Pity too and grace, \\
        Softly lustred all Thy dealing \\
            With our stricken race. \\
        Thou with sympathy and healing \\
            'Mid our woes didst move, \\
        Every gracious deed revealing \\
            Thou, O Lord, art love.

    \item E'en when in Thy poignant anguish, \\
            Dying on the tree, \\
        When men left Thee there to languish, \\
            Full of hate to Thee, \\
        When our sins and judgment bearing, \\
            Smitten from above, \\
        Every cry was then declaring, \\
            Thou, O Lord, art love.

    \item Seated in triumphant glory \\
            On the Father's throne, \\
        Still we read the self-same story \\
            That on earth was known; \\
        Changeless in Thy holy dwelling \\
            Thou dost ever prove; \\
        There Thy wound-prints aye are telling \\
            Thou, O Lord, art love.

    \item Thou, Lord, in Thy heart's affection \\
            Pleadest for us there; \\
        Knowing well our imperfection, \\
            Thou our woes canst share; \\
        Thou canst succour in temptation, \\
            Strengthen from above, \\
        Comfort give and consolation; \\
            Thou, O Lord, art love.

    \item Payment meet for all we owe Thee, \\
            We, O Lord, have none; \\
        But we long still more to know Thee \\
            Who our hearts hast won. \\
        May we here, Thy love compelling, \\
            Ever grateful prove; \\
        Walk and word and action telling, \\
            Thou, O Lord, art love.

    \item Soon, enraptured we shall meet Thee; \\
            Thou for us wilt come; \\
        Face to face with joy we'll greet Thee \\
            In that heavenly home. \\
        There, where angel-throngs are praising \\
            We with Thee shall move, \\
        This eternal anthem raising--- \\
            Thou, O Lord, art love.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 207}}
    \\
    \textit{C. M. SAWLEY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item O LORD, when we the path retrace \\
        Which Thou on earth hast trod, \\
        To man Thy wondrous love and grace, \\
        Thy faithfulness to God;

    \item Thy love to man, so sorely tried, \\
        Proved stronger than the grave; \\
        The very spear that pierced Thy side \\
        Drew forth the blood to save;

    \item Faithful amidst unfaithfulness, \\
        'Midst darkness only light, \\
        Thou didst Thy Father's name confess, \\
        And in His will delight;

    \item Unmoved by Satan's subtle wiles, \\
        By suffering, shame and loss; \\
        Thy path, uncheered by earthly smiles, \\
        Led only to the cross;

    \item O Lord, with sorrow and with shame, \\
        We meekly would confess \\
        How little we, who bear Thy name, \\
        Thy mind, Thy will express.

    \item Give us Thy meek, Thy lowly mind; \\
        We would obedient be, \\
        And all our rest and pleasure find \\
        In fellowship with Thee.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 208}}
    \\
    \textit{C. M. SAWLEY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item WHAT grace, O Lord, and beauty shone \\
        Around Thy steps below! \\
        What patient love was seen in all \\
        Thy life and death of woe!

    \item For ever on Thy burdened heart \\
        A weight of sorrow hung; \\
        Yet no ungentle, murmuring word \\
        Escaped Thy silent tongue.

    \item Thy foes might hate, despise, revile, \\
        Thy friends unfaithful prove; \\
        Unwearied in forgiveness still, \\
        Thy heart could only love.

    \item Oh, give us hearts to love like Thee, \\
        Like Thee, O Lord, to grieve \\
        Far more for others' sins, than all \\
        The wrongs that we receive.

    \item One with Thyself, may every eye \\
        In us, Thy brethren, see \\
        That gentleness and grace that spring \\
        From union, Lord, with Thee.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 209}}
    \\
    \textit{Irregular PEACE! BE STILL!}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item MASTER, the tempest is raging, \\
            The billows are tossing high, \\
        The sky is o'ershadowed with blackness, \\
            No shelter or help is nigh. \\
        Carest Thou not that we perish? \\
            How canst Thou lie asleep, \\
        When each moment so madly is threatening \\
            A grave in the angry deep?

    \item[] \vspace{0.4em}
    \textbf{\textit{[Chorus]}} \\
    \textit{The winds and the waves shall obey My will,} \\
    \textit{    Peace, be still!} \\
    \textit{Whether the wrath of the storm-tossed sea,} \\
    \textit{Or demons, or men, or whatever it be,} \\
    \textit{No waters can swallow the ship where lies} \\
    \textit{The Master of ocean, and earth, and skies;} \\
    \textit{They all shall sweetly obey My will,} \\
    \textit{    Peace, be still!}
    \vspace{0.4em}

    \item Master, the terror is over, \\
            The elements sweetly rest. \\
        In calmness the lake now reposes, \\
            And peacefulness fills each breast. \\
        Master, almighty Peacegiver, \\
            Speak to the sin-tossed soul \\
        Who cries to Thee, "Save, or I perish," \\
            Deliver and take control.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 210}}
    \\
    \textit{11.10.11.10. STRENGTH AND STAY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item PROPITIOUS seemed the gentle waters laving \\
            Gennes'ret's shore when first the little band, \\
        All thought for coming storm and danger waiving, \\
            Set forth to cross it at their Lord's command.

    \item But soon the scene in tempest darkness merges, \\
            The water rages, torn by shrieking winds; \\
        Faith staggers with the boat, and upmost surges \\
            O'erwhelming fear within the tremblers' minds.

    \item "Dost Thou not care, O Master, that we perish, \\
            Who at Thy word embarked upon this sea? \\
        Canst Thou at such a moment slumber cherish, \\
            Indifferent, though we die because of Thee?"

    \item Calm and majestic rises from His pillow \\
            Sea's mighty Lord, commanding, "Peace, be still!" \\
        Sink then to rest, with troubled wind and billow, \\
            Their tossing minds, soothed by His potent will.

    \item Disciples all! The Lord Himself has set us \\
            The course by which we voyage to our home; \\
        Here lies our confidence whate'er beset us, \\
            We sail with One whose power must overcome.

    \item 'Tis easy when o'er stilly waters gliding, \\
            In peaceful faith upon the Lord to rest; \\
        But when our bark on troublous waves is riding, \\
            Will faith triumphant issue through the test?

    \item Calm mid surrounding turmoil, mighty Master, \\
            Teach us to rest secure beneath Thy wing; \\
        Thine arm will guard Thy weak ones from disaster, \\
            And safely to their longed-for haven bring.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 211}}
    \\
    \textit{L. M. GIESSEN}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderately fast]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item RIDE on, ride on, in majesty; \\
        Hark! all the tribes "Hosanna" cry. \\
        O Saviour meek, pursue Thy road \\
        With palms and scattered garments strewed. \\
            Ride on, ride on, in majesty, \\
            In lowly pomp ride on to die.

    \item Ride on, ride on, in majesty; \\
        The angel armies of the sky \\
        Look down with sad and wondering eyes, \\
        To see th'approaching sacrifice.

    \item Ride on, ride on, in majesty; \\
        The last and fiercest strife is nigh; \\
        The Father on His sapphire throne \\
        Awaits His own anointed Son.

    \item Ride on, ride on, in majesty, \\
        In lowly pomp ride on to die; \\
        Bow Thy meek head to mortal pain, \\
        Then take, O God, Thy power, and reign.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 212}}
    \\
    \textit{8.3.8.3.8.8.8.3. GOLGOTHA}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item BEHOLD, behold the Lamb of God, \\
            On the cross. \\
        For us He shed His precious blood, \\
            On the cross. \\
        Oh, hear His awful, anguished cry, \\
        "Eli, lama sabachthani;" \\
        Draw near and see the Saviour die \\
            On the cross.

    \item Behold His arms extended wide, \\
            On the cross. \\
        Behold His bleeding hands and side, \\
            On the cross. \\
        The sun withholds its rays of light, \\
        The heavens are clothed in shades of night, \\
        While Jesus fights the dreadful fight \\
            On the cross.

    \item By faith we see Him lifted up, \\
            On the cross. \\
        He drinks for us the bitter cup, \\
            On the cross. \\
        The rocks are rent, the mountains quake, \\
        While Jesus doth atonement make, \\
        While Jesus suffers for our sake \\
            On the cross.

    \item And now the mighty deed is done, \\
            On the cross. \\
        The battle's fought, the victory won, \\
            On the cross. \\
        To heaven He turns His holy eyes, \\
        "'Tis finished," now the Conqueror cries, \\
        Then bows His sacred head and dies \\
            On the cross.

    \item Where'er I go, I'll tell the story \\
            Of the cross. \\
        In nothing else my soul shall glory, \\
            Save the cross. \\
        Yes, this my constant theme shall be, \\
        Through time and in eternity--- \\
        How Jesus conquered death for me \\
            On the cross.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 213}}
    \\
    \textit{8.7.8.7. D. AUSTRIA}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item SWEET the moments, rich in blessing, \\
            Which before the cross we spend, \\
        Life and health and peace possessing, \\
            From the sinner's dying Friend. \\
        Here we rest, in wonder viewing \\
            All our sins on Jesus laid, \\
        And a full redemption flowing \\
            From the sacrifice He made.

    \item Truly bless\`ed is the station, \\
            Low before the cross to lie, \\
        And behold the great salvation \\
            To rebellious man brought nigh. \\
        Here we find the dawn of heav\`en, \\
            While upon the cross we gaze, \\
        See our trespasses forgiv\`en, \\
            And our songs of triumph raise.

    \item Oh that near the cross abiding \\
            We may to the Saviour cleave, \\
        Nought from Him our hearts dividing, \\
            All for Him content to leave. \\
        May we still, the cross discerning, \\
            There for peace and comfort go, \\
        There new wonders daily learning, \\
            All the depths of mercy know.

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
    {\Large \textbf{HYMN 214}}
    \\
    \textit{C. M. HORSLEY}
    \\
    \textit{\small [Moderate]}
\end{center}
\vspace{1em}

\begin{enumerate}
    \item THERE is a green hill far away, \\
            Outside a city wall, \\
        Where Christ the Lord was crucified, \\
            Who died to save us all.

    \item We do not know, we cannot tell \\
            The pains He had to bear; \\
        But this we know, it was for us \\
            He bled and suffered there.

    \item He died that we might be forgiven, \\
            That we so void of good \\
        Might ever be with Him in heaven, \\
            Saved by His precious blood.

    \item There was no other good enough \\
            To pay the price of sin; \\
        He only could unlock the gate \\
            Of heaven, to let us in.

    \item Oh dearly, dearly has He loved! \\
            Shall we not love Him too, \\
        Who trust in His redeeming blood, \\
            And seek His will to do?

\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\begin{center}
